evel=4,text="Numbers",content={[1]="",[2]="[max](max/main.md)",[3]="",[4]="[min](min/main.md)",[5]=""},children={}}}},[4]={level=3,text="Memory",content={[1]="",[2]="[unmap](unmap/main.md)",[3]="",[4]="[peek8](peek8/main.md)",[5]="",[6]="[peek4](peek4/main.md)",[7]="",[8]="[peek2](peek2/main.md)",[9]="",[10]="[peek](peek/main.md)",[11]="",[12]="[poke8](poke8/main.md)",[13]="",[14]="[poke4](poke4/main.md)",[15]="",[16]="[poke2](poke2/main.md)",[17]="",[18]="[poke](poke/main.md)",[19]="",[20]="[memset](memset/main.md)",[21]="",[22]="[memmap](memmap/main.md)",[23]="",[24]="[memcpy](memcpy/main.md)",[25]=""},children={}},[5]={level=3,text="Graphics",content={[1]="",[2]="[spr](spr/main.md)",[3]="",[4]="[sspr](sspr/main.md)",[5]="",[6]="[set_spr](set_spr/main.md)",[7]="",[8]="[set_draw_target](set_draw_target/main.md)",[9]="",[10]="[rect](rect/main.md)",[11]="",[12]="[rectfill](rectfill/main.md)",[13]="",[14]="[rrect](rrect/main.md)",[15]="",[16]="[rrectfill](rrectfill/main.md)",[17]="",[18]="[oval](oval/main.md)",[19]="",[20]="[ovalfill](ovalfill/main.md)",[21]="",[22]="[line](line/main.md)",[23]="",[24]="[color](color/main.md)",[25]="",[26]="[cls](cls/main.md)",[27]="",[28]="[clip](clip/main.md)",[29]="",[30]="[circ](circ/main.md)",[31]="",[32]="[circfill](circfill/main.md)",[33]="",[34]="[camera](camera/main.md)",[35]="",[36]="[fillp](fillp/main.md)",[37]="",[38]="[get_spr](get_spr/main.md)",[39]="",[40]="[get_draw_target](get_draw_target/main.md)",[41]="",[42]="[get_display](get_display/main.md)",[43]="",[44]="[print](print/main.md)",[45]="",[46]="[pset](pset/main.md)",[47]="",[48]="[pget](pget/main.md)",[49]=""},children={}},[6]={level=3,text="Audio",content={[1]="",[2]="[sfx](sfx/main.md)",[3]="",[4]="[note](note/main.md)",[5]="",[6]="[music](music/main.md)",[7]=""},children={}},[7]={level=3,text="Processes",content={[1]="",[2]="[create_process](create_process/main.md)",[3]="",[4]="[env](env/main.md)",[5]="",[6]="[send_message](send_message/main.md)",[7]="",[8]="[on a window and/or set the window's attributes.",[3]="",[4]="Source: [source.lua](source.lua)",[5]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="[width,height]",content={[1]="",[2]="Proportions of the window",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=3,text="attribs",content={[1]="",[2]="Table of desired attributes of the window",[3]="",[4]="Cannot overwrite `parent_pid`",[5]=""},children={[1]={level=4,text="`width`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="width in pixels (not including the frame)",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=4,text="`height`: height",content={[1]="",[2]="height in pixels",[3]=""},children={}},[3]={level=4,text="`title`: string",content={[1]="",[2]="set a title displayed on the window's titlebar",[3]=""},children={}},[4]={level=4,text="`pauseable`: boolean",content={[1]="",[2]="false to turn off the app menu that normally comes up with ENTER/START button",[3]=""},children={}},[5]={level=4,text="`tabbed`: boolean",content={[1]="",[2]="true to open in a tabbed workspace (like the code editor)",[3]=""},children={}},[6]={level=4,text="`has_frame`: boolean",content={[1]="",[2]="Default: true",[3]="",[4]="Whether to have a frame around the window",[5]=""},children={}},[7]={level=4,text="`moveable`: boolean",content={[1]="",[2]="Default: true",[3]="",[4]="Whether to allow the window to be dragged",[5]=""},children={}},[8]={level=4,text="`resizeable`: boolean",content={[1]="",[2]="Default: true",[3]="",[4]="Whether to allow the window to be resized",[5]=""},children={}},[9]={level=4,text="`wallpaper`: boolean",content={[1]="",[2]="Default: true",[3]="",[4]="acts as a wallpaper:",[5]="* z defaults to -1000 in that case",[6]="* opens a filenav (for the desktop interface)",[7]=""},children={}},[10]={level=4,text="`autoclose`: boolean",content={[1]="",[2]="close window when is no longer in focus or when press escape",[3]=""},children={}},[11]={level=4,text="`x`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="Position of the window",[3]=""},children={}},[12]={level=4,text="`y`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="Position of the window",[3]=""},children={}},[13]={level=4,text="`z`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="Default: 0",[3]="",[4]="windows with higher z are drawn on top.",[5]=""},children={}},[14]={level=4,text="`cursor`: string|number",content={[1]="",[2]="0 for no cursor",[3]="",[4]="1 for Default",[5]="",[6]="userdata for a custom cursor",[7]="",[8]="Use strings for some built in cursors, e.g: `pointer`",[9]=""},children={}},[15]={level=4,text="`squashable`: boolean",content={[1]="",[2]="window resizes itself to stay within the desktop region",[3]=""},children={}},[16]={level=4,text="`background_updates`: boolean",content={[1]="",[2]="allow _update() callbacks when parent window is not visible",[3]=""},children={}},[17]={level=4,text="`background_draws`: boolean",content={[1]="",[2]="allow _draw() callbacks when parent window is not visible",[3]=""},children={}}}},[3]={level=3,text="`show_context_menu`: boolean",content={[1]="",[2]="displays the menu at the user's cursor",[3]=""},children={}},[4]={level=3,text="`ghost`: boolean",content={[1]="",[2]="Window is drawn, uninteractable, inputs passthrough to the below window"},children={}}}}}}}},[140]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/vid/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="vid(mode)",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="Sets the display size and hides the cursor.",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`mode`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="One of 5 modes (`0..4`).",[3]="",[4]="0. 480x270",[5]="1. 320x180 (NOT IMPLEMENTED; planned for 0.2.4)",[6]="2. 240x180 (NOT IMPLEMENTED; planned for 0.2.4)",[7]="3. 240x135",[8]="4. 160x190"},children={}}}}}}}},[141]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/vec/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="vec(...): ud",content={},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="The vec function is one of two ways to create a new userdata from scratch, with the other being the [userdata](/picotron_api/functions/userdata/main.md) function.",[2]="Produces a new f64 typed 1D [userdata](/picotron_api/userdata/readme.md) populated with each of the arguments.",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={},children={[1]={level=3,text="`...`: number|nil",content={[1]="Each value to populate the userdata with. The number of arguments dictates the width of the produced userdata. Defaults to 0.",[2]=""},children={}}}},[3]={level=2,text="Returns",content={},children={[1]={level=3,text="`ud`: [userdata](/picotron_api/userdata/readme.md)|nil",content={[1]="The userdata produced, or nil if no arguments were provided.",[2]=""},children={}}}},[4]={level=2,text="Example",content={[1]="```lua",[2]="local position = vec(12, 19)",[3]="",[4]="?position:get(0) -- 12",[5]="?position:get(1) -- 19",[6]="```",[7]=""},children={}}}}}},[142]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/userdata/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="userdata(type, width, height, [data]): ud",content={},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="The userdata function is one of two ways to create a new userdata from scratch, with the other being the [vec](/picotron_api/functions/vec/main.md) function.",[2]="Produces a new 2D [userdata](/picotron_api/userdata/readme.md) with a specified type and size. 2D userdatas with a height of 1 are not the same as 1D userdatas, and will cause certain functions, such as [`get`](/picotron_api/userdata/methods/get/main.md) and [`set`](/picotron_api/userdata/methods/set/main.md), to behave differently.",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={},children={[1]={level=3,text="`type`: \"u8\"|\"i16\"|\"i32\"|\"i64\"|\"f64\"",content={[1]="",[2]="The type of number that the userdata will contain.",[3]="- \"u8\" is an unsigned 8-bit integer",[4]="- \"i16\" is a signed 16-bit integer",[5]="- \"i32\" is a signed 32-bit integer",[6]="- \"i64\" is a signed 64-bit integer",[7]="- \"f64\" is a 64-bit floating point number",[8]=""},children={}},[2]={level=3,text="`width`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="The number of columns in the produced userdata. Will be floored. If less than 1, causes the returned value to be nil.",[3]=""},children={}},[3]={level=3,text="`height`: number",content={[1]="The number of rows in the produced userdata. Will be floored. If less than 1, causes the returned value to be nil.",[2]=""},children={}},[4]={level=3,text="`[data]`: string",content={[1]="The values to initialize the userdata with. Defaults to setting every entry to 0.",[2]="",[3]="If the userdata is an integer type, `data` must be formatted as a contiguous 0-padded hexidecimal string. For instance, if `type` is `i16`, the string `\"000F2001\"` would produce the numbers (in decimal) 15 and 8,193 for indices 0 and 1.",[4]="",[5]="If the userdata is a floating point type, `data` must be formatted as a comma-separated list of decimal values. For instance, `\"0.1,0.5,12\"`.",[6]=""},children={}}}},[3]={level=2,text="Returns",content={},children={[1]={level=3,text="`ud`: [userdata](/picotron_api/userdata/readme.md)|nil",content={[1]="The userdata produced, or nil if `width` or `height` is less than 1.",[2]=""},children={}}}},[4]={level=2,text="Example",content={[1]="```lua",[2]="local ud = userdata(\"u8\", 2, 2, \"080D1522\")",[3]="",[4]="?ud:get(0, 0) -- 8",[5]="?ud:get(1, 0) -- 13",[6]="?ud:get(0, 1) -- 21",[7]="?ud:get(1, 1) -- 34",[8]="```",[9]=""},children={}}}},[2]={level=1,text="userdata(type, width, [data]): ud",content={},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="The userdata function is one of two ways to create a new userdata from scratch, with the other being the [vec](/picotron_api/functions/vec/main.md) function.",[2]="Produces a new 1D [userdata](/picotron_api/userdata/readme.md) with a specified type and size. 1D userdatas are not the same as 2D userdatas with a height of 1, and will cause certain functions, such as [`get`](/picotron_api/userdata/methods/get/main.md) and [`set`](/picotron_api/userdata/methods/set/main.md), to behave differently.",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={},children={[1]={level=3,text="`type`: \"u8\"|\"i16\"|\"i32\"|\"i64\"|\"f64\"",content={[1]="",[2]="The type of number that the userdata will contain.",[3]="- \"u8\" is an unsigned 8-bit integer",[4]="- \"i16\" is a signed 16-bit integer",[5]="- \"i32\" is a signed 32-bit integer",[6]="- \"i64\" is a signed 64-bit integer",[7]="- \"f64\" is a 64-bit floating point number",[8]=""},children={}},[2]={level=3,text="`width`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="The number of columns in the produced userdata. Will be floored. If less than 1, causes the returned value to be nil.",[3]=""},children={}},[3]={level=3,text="`[data]`: string",content={[1]="The values to initialize the userdata with. Defaults to setting every entry to 0.",[2]="",[3]="If the userdata is an integer type, `data` must be formatted as a contiguous 0-padded hexidecimal string. For instance, if `type` is `i16`, the string `\"000F2001\"` would produce the numbers (in decimal) 15 and 8,193 for indices 0 and 1.",[4]="",[5]="If the userdata is a floating point type, `data` must be formatted as a comma-separated list of decimal values. For instance, `\"0.1,0.5,12\"`.",[6]=""},children={}}}},[3]={level=2,text="Returns",content={},children={[1]={level=3,text="`ud`: [userdata](/picotron_api/userdata/readme.md)|nil",content={[1]="The userdata produced, or nil if `width` is less than 1.",[2]=""},children={}}}},[4]={level=2,text="Example",content={[1]="```lua",[2]="local ud = userdata(\"u8\", 4, \"080D1522\")",[3]="",[4]="?ud:get(0) -- 8",[5]="?ud:get(1) -- 13",[6]="?ud:get(2) -- 21",[7]="?ud:get(3) -- 34",[8]="```",[9]=""},children={}}}}}},[143]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/unpod/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="unpod(str): any, [table]",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="`unpod` returns the decoded value, and the decoded metadata as a second result.",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`str`: string",content={[1]="",[2]="The encoded string, generated by [`pod()`](/picotron_api/functions/pod/main.md).",[3]=""},children={}}}},[3]={level=2,text="Returns",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`value`: any",content={[1]="",[2]="The value that was podded in `str`.",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=3,text="`metadata`: [table]",content={[1]="",[2]="The metadata, if present, of the encoded string.",[3]=""},children={}}}},[4]={level=2,text="Examples",content={[1]="",[2]="Sourced from the [Picotron Manual](https://www.lexaloffle.com/dl/docs/picotron_manual.html)",[3]="",[4]="```lua",[5]="str = pod({4,5,6}, {desc = \"an uninteresting sequence\"})",[6]=" ",[7]="content,metadata = unpod(str) -- returns content and metadata",[8]="?metadata.desc --  an uninteresting sequence",[9]="?content[1] -- 4",[10]="```"},children={}}}}}},[144]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/unmap/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="unmap(ud,addr,[len])",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="this is the only way to release mapped userdata for collection",[3]="",[4]="e.g. memmapping a userdata over an old one is not sufficient to free it for collection",[5]="",[6]="Source: [source.lua](source.lua)",[7]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="ud",content={[1]="",[2]="The userdata that is unmapped from memory",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=3,text="addr",content={[1]="",[2]="The address to unmap the userdata from",[3]=""},children={}},[3]={level=3,text="[len]",content={[1]="",[2]="Length of the userdata to unmap, defaults to the full userdata length",[3]=""},children={}}}},[3]={level=2,text="Returns",content={[1]="",[2]="This function does not return anything"},children={}}}}}},[145]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/type/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="type(value): string",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="`type` returns the type of a value in the form of a string.",[3]="",[4]="For whether a number is an `integer` or a `float`, make use of `math.type(num)`.",[5]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="value",content={[1]="",[2]="The value to query the type of",[3]=""},children={}}}},[3]={level=2,text="Returns",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`type`: string",content={[1]="",[2]="The type of the value `value`, possibilities being:",[3]="",[4]="* string (`\"hello world\"`)",[5]="* number (`2`)",[6]="* function (`print`)",[7]="* boolean (`true`)",[8]="* nil (`nil`)",[9]="* table (`{}`)",[10]="* userdata",[11]="* thread"},children={}}}}}}}},[146]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/tokenoid/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="tokenoid(line,pos,[token_state]): str, pos1, category, new_token_state",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="`tokenoid` returns the tokenisation of a string in lua into 6 categories: spaces, regular text, numbers, comments, strings and operators.",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`line`: string",content={[1]="",[2]="The line of lua code to process.",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=3,text="`pos`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="The character index of the line to process from",[3]=""},children={}},[3]={level=3,text="`[token_state]`: unknown|nil",content={[1]="",[2]="The previous token state; used to support things such as multi-line strings and multi-line comments.",[3]=""},children={}}}},[3]={level=2,text="Returns",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`str`: string",content={[1]="",[2]="The string being the lua token that was processed, e.g: `apple` in `function apple()`.",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=3,text="`pos1`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="The starting position of the next token.",[3]="",[4]="When `pos` is more than the length of `line`, it is the end of the last token extracted.",[5]=""},children={}},[3]={level=3,text="`category`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="The category of the token, these are as follows:",[3]="",[4]="0. Space",[5]="1. Regular text",[6]="2. Number",[7]="3. Comment",[8]="4. String",[9]="5. Operators",[10]=""},children={}},[4]={level=3,text="`new_token_state`: unknown",content={[1]="",[2]="The new token state, used for multi-line comments and strings.",[3]=""},children={}}}},[4]={level=2,text="Examples",content={[1]="",[2]="A simple Lua syntaxer.",[3]="",[4]="This can be seen in the example lua syntaxer cart found on the [bbs](https://www.lexaloffle.com/bbs/?tid=155528).",[5]="",[6]="The png file can be downloaded from here ![syntaxer cartridge](syntaxer.p64.png)",[7]="",[8]="```lua",[9]="local syntaxes={",[10]="	[0]=\"7\", --space",[11]="	[1]=\"7\", --regular text",[12]="	[2]=\"c\", --number",[13]="	[3]=\"d\", --comment",[14]="	[4]=\"c\", --string",[15]="	[5]=\"7\" --operator",[16]="}",[17]="",[18]="function syntax_line(line,token_state)",[19]="	local pos=1",[20]="	local out=\"\"",[21]="	while pos<=#line do",[22]="		--use tokenoid to handle main syntax",[23]="		local str,pos1,cat,new_state=tokenoid(line,pos,token_state)",[24]="		if (not str) break",[25]="		",[26]="		local col=syntaxes[cat] or 7",[27]="		",[28]="		--now do strings like \"function\", \"end\", \"then\", \"break\", etcetc",[29]="		",[30]="		if (cat==1) then",[31]="			if (",[32]="				str==\"function\" or str==\"end\" or str==\"return\"",[33]="				or str==\"local\"",[34]="				",[35]="				or str==\"for\" or str==\"while\"",[36]="				or str==\"in\" or str==\"do\"",[37]="				or str==\"if\" or str==\"break\" or str==\"then\" or str==\"else\"",[38]="				or str==\"or\"",[39]="				) then",[40]="				col=\"e\"",[41]="			end",[42]="		end",[43]="		out..=\"\\\"f\"..col..str",[44]="		",[45]="		pos=pos1",[46]="		token_state=new_state",[47]="	end",[48]="	",[49]="	return out,token_state",[50]="end",[51]="",[52]="function syntax(text)",[53]="	text=text:split(\"\\\"n\")",[54]="	local res=\"\"",[55]="	local l,token_state=\"\",nil",[56]="	for i,line in ipairs(text) do",[57]="		l,token_state=syntax_line(line,token_state)",[58]="		res..=l..\"\\\"n\"",[59]="	end",[60]="	return res",[61]="end",[62]="",[63]="txt=[[n=1",[64]="function foo()",[65]="	n+=1",[66]="end",[67]="",[68]="foo()",[69]="print(n)]]",[70]="",[71]="print(syntax(txt))",[72]="```",[73]="",[74]="This can be paired with the api functions to create a full lua syntaxer for picotron.",[75]="",[76]="```lua",[77]="local syntaxes={",[78]="	[0]=\"7\", --space",[79]="	[1]=\"7\", --regular text",[80]="	[2]=\"c\", --number",[81]="	[3]=\"d\", --comment",[82]="	[4]=\"c\", --string",[83]="	[5]=\"7\" --operator",[84]="}",[85]="",[86]="function syntax_line(line,token_state)",[87]="	local pos=1",[88]="	local out=\"\"",[89]="	while pos<=#line do",[90]="		--use tokenoid to handle main syntax",[91]="		local str,pos1,cat,new_state=tokenoid(line,pos,token_state)",[92]="		if (not str) break",[93]="		",[94]="		local col=syntaxes[cat] or 7",[95]="		",[96]="		--now do strings like \"function\", \"end\", \"then\", \"break\", etcetc",[97]="		",[98]="		if (cat==1) then",[99]="			if (",[100]="				str==\"function\" or str==\"end\" or str==\"return\"",[101]="				or str==\"local\"",[102]="				",[103]="				or str==\"for\" or str==\"while\"",[104]="				or str==\"in\" or str==\"do\"",[105]="				or str==\"if\" or str==\"break\" or str==\"then\" or str==\"else\"",[106]="				or str==\"or\"",[107]="				) then",[108]="				col=\"e\"",[109]="			else",[110]="				local api={",[111]="					flr=1,pset=1,select=1,set_spr=1,vec=1,set=1,map=1,reset=1,palt=1,userdata=1,poke8=1,get_spr=1,",[112]="					get=1,cp=1,socket=1,foreach=1,t=1,create_process=1,pal=1,sspr=1,tokenoid=1,fillp=1,",[113]="					memset=1,norm=1,btn=1,assert=1,type=1,oval=1,create_undo_stack=1,error=1,poke2=1,",[114]="					theme=1,add=1,env=1,fetch_metadata=1,clear_key=1,pwf=1,menuitem=1,load=1,create_gui=1,",[115]="					on_event=1,sfx=1,mv=1,store=1,tonum=1,mouselock=1,pwd=1,music=1,print=1,memmap=1,store_metadata=1,",[116]="					tostring=1,time=1,pod=1,include=1,rect=1,date=1,stat=1,get_clipboard=1,peektext=1,split=1,",[117]="					pairs=1,unpod=1,readtext=1,key=1,getmetatable=1,camera=1,keyp=1,sgn=1,fset=1,mouse=1,tostr=1,",[118]="					notify=1,all=1,sub=1,rawlen=1,ord=1,mset=1,yield=1,pack=1,costatus=1,cocreate=1,min=1,ceil=1,",[119]="					rawequal=1,unmap=1,coresume=1,stop=1,peek2=1,unpack=1,printh=1,exit=1,memcpy=1,note=1,window=1,",[120]="					line=1,get_display=1,cursor=1,get_draw_target=1,open=1,pget=1,del=1,mid=1,spr=1,ipairs=1,",[121]="					vid=1,rawset=1,peek4=1,poke=1,deli=1,peek=1,btnp=1,pid=1,tonumber=1,circfill=1,rrectfill=1,",[122]="					max=1,set_draw_target=1,cd=1,mget=1,cls=1,mount=1,poke4=1,color=1,blit=1,fstat=1,atan2=1,",[123]="					collectgarbage=1,ls=1,setmetatable=1,rm=1,send_message=1,count=1,mkdir=1,create_delta=1,",[124]="					cos=1,rawget=1,circ=1,clip=1,flip=1,next=1,peek8=1,fetch=1,wrangle_working_file=1,tline3d=1,",[125]="					rectfill=1,srand=1,set_clipboard=1,fget=1,sqrt=1,warn=1,pcall=1,fullpath=1,chr=1,apply_delta=1,",[126]="					rnd=1,ovalfill=1,abs=1,sin=1,rrect=1,[\"goto\"]=1",[127]="				}",[128]="				if (api[str]) col=\"b\"",[129]="			end",[130]="		end",[131]="		out..=\"\\\"f\"..col..str",[132]="		",[133]="		pos=pos1",[134]="		token_state=new_state",[135]="	end",[136]="	",[137]="	return out,token_state",[138]="end",[139]="",[140]="function syntax(text)",[141]="	text=text:split(\"\\\"n\")",[142]="	local res=\"\"",[143]="	local l,token_state=\"\",nil",[144]="	for i,line in ipairs(text) do",[145]="		l,token_state=syntax_line(line,token_state)",[146]="		res..=l..\"\\\"n\"",[147]="	end",[148]="	return res",[149]="end",[150]="",[151]="txt=[[n=1",[152]="function foo()",[153]="	n+=1",[154]="end",[155]="",[156]="foo()",[157]="print(n)]]",[158]="",[159]="print(syntax(txt))",[160]="```"},children={}}}}}},[147]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/time/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="time(): number",content={},children={}},[2]={level=1,text="t(): number",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="`time` returns the amount of seconds since the process has started running. This can become inaccurate as it is relative to `_update`, so if the process lags; the time may be incorrect.",[3]="",[4]="You can make use of [stat(86)](/picotron_api/functions/stat/main.md) for an accurate timestamp which can be compared with the starting timestamp of the program.",[5]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Returns",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`time`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="The time since the process has started running, relative to the process's update cycle."},children={}}}}}}}},[148]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/sub/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="sub(str,pos0,pos1): string",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="`sub` returns a substring of the string `sub`, similar to Lua's default [`string.sub`](https://www.lua.org/pil/20.html) (which is also available in Picotron).",[2]="",[3]="This can also be used in the form of `string:sub()`, where `string` becomes the first argument, `str`.",[4]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`str`: string",content={[1]="The string to acquire the substring of.",[2]=""},children={}},[2]={level=3,text="`pos0`: integer",content={[1]="The first position of the substring (character index).",[2]="",[3]="Positions are 1-indexed, similar to tables, so the first character is at index 1.",[4]=""},children={}},[3]={level=3,text="`pos1`: integer|nil",content={[1]="The second position of the substring (character index), if this is `nil`, it defaults to the value in `pos0`.",[2]="",[3]="Positions are 1-indexed, similar to tables, so the first character is at index 1.",[4]=""},children={}}}},[3]={level=2,text="Returns",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`substring`: string",content={[1]="The substring of the string.",[2]=""},children={}}}},[4]={level=2,text="Example",content={[1]="```lua",[2]="local string=\"Hello world\"",[3]="",[4]="?sub(string,1,5) -- \"Hello\"",[5]="?sub(string,7) -- \"World\"",[6]="?string:sub(1,5) -- \"H\"",[7]="```"},children={}}}}}},[149]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/store_metadata/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="store_metadata(filename,meta)",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="`store_metadata` stores metadata, `meta`, at a file or directory, `filename`, merging with the original metadata.",[3]="",[4]="Source: [source.lua](source.lua)",[5]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="filename",content={[1]="",[2]="The path to the file or directory to store metadata in",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=3,text="meta",content={[1]="",[2]="The metadata to store in the file or directory",[3]=""},children={}}}},[3]={level=2,text="Returns",content={[1]="",[2]="This function does not return anything",[3]=""},children={}},[4]={level=2,text="Possible Future",content={[1]="",[2]="Ability to remove metadata items from the pre-existing metadata"},children={}}}}}},[150]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/store/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="store(location, obj, [metadata])",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="`store` allows you to store files, typically used for save files by storing tables in the `pod` file format.",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`location`: string",content={[1]="",[2]="The location you want to store a file at, e.g: `/appdata/mypod.pod`",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=3,text="`obj`: any",content={[1]="",[2]="The file data, e.g: a table for a `.pod` file.",[3]=""},children={}},[3]={level=3,text="`[metadata]`: table",content={[1]="",[2]="Optional metadata of the file, e.g: `{mymetadatavalue=\"grapes\"}`",[3]="",[4]="If you use `{metadata_format=\"none\"}`, this will cut any metadata from the file."},children={}}}}}}}},[151]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/stop/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="stop([message])",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="`stop` halts the program and prints a message to the terminal.",[3]="",[4]="This is meant for use during development; unlike [error()](/picotron_api/functions/error/main.md), this simply suspends the program and can be resumed by the terminal with [`resume`](/system/details/util/resume/main.md) if it is the present working cartridge.",[5]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`[message]`: string",content={[1]="",[2]="A message that is printed to the terminal"},children={}}}}}}}},[152]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/stat/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="function stat(n,[...])",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="Greatly helped by [@akd-io and @Maxine](https://github.com/akd-io/picotron/blob/ccb6d34d8594cd4c2d45930cf8723c241f5aa6e9/drive/projects/stat/stats.md?plain=1#L80)",[3]="Returns information about the system and can sometimes do actions such as garbage collection.",[4]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="n: `number`",content={[1]="",[2]="The id for the information you want to fetch",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=3,text="[...]: `arbritrary`",content={[1]="",[2]="Arbritrary arguments, sometimes used in stat, e.g: with `stat(302,keycode)`",[3]=""},children={}},[3]={level=3,text="Valid usage",content={[1]="",[2]="Where the first number is `n` and any other arguments, if present, are `[...]`",[3]="",[4]="`Unknown` signifies that it returns values, but what they mean is unknown.",[5]="",[6]="@Astralsparv's [Picotron Logger Distribution](https://github.com/Astralsparv/Picotron-Logger-Distribution) will be updated to help document this, fed the arbritrary values seen in unknown stats and detecting any change in the stat per frame in hopes of finding a cause and reproducing it.",[7]=""},children={[1]={level=4,text="Stats",content={[1]="where c is a channel, addr is an address, n is a node:",[2]="",[3]="* 0 - memory usage (bytes), also triggers a garbage collection",[4]="* 1 - cpu usage (decimal)",[5]="* 2 - reserved",[6]="* 3 - raw memory usage (bytes), no garbage collection",[7]="* 5 - runtime, system version",[8]="     `runtime,system_version=stat(5)`",[9]="* 7 - operating fps (60, 30, 20, 15)",[10]="* 64 - user_id of the currently logged in lexaloffle account",[11]="* 65 - username of the currently logged in lexaloffle account",[12]="* 66 - userdata u8 sprite of the currently logged in lexaloffle account",[13]="* 70 - table of the user ids of lexaloffle accounts have been logged into",[14]="* 86 - epoch time",[15]="* 87 - timezone delta (seconds)",[16]="* 101 - web: player cart id (when playing a bbs cart; nil otherwise)",[17]="* 150 - web: window.location.href",[18]="* 151 - web: stat(150) up to the end of the window.location.pathname",[19]="* 152 - web: window.location.host",[20]="* 153 - web: window.location.hash",[21]="* 301 - total CPU usage?",[22]="* 304 - system CPU usage",[23]="* 302, keycode - returns human readable name for the keycode, surface [SDL's GetKeyName](https://wiki.libsdl.org/SDL2/SDL_GetKeyName)",[24]="* 305 - Whether any key is pressed",[25]="* 307 - 1.0 if current working path is `/system` (including if the cartridge is located in `/system/`)",[26]="* 308 - Unknown",[27]="* 309 - Unknown",[28]="* 310 - Unknown",[29]="* 311 - Unknown",[30]="* 312 - 4096 (amount of bytes in a page)",[31]="* 313 - Unknown, *likely* amount of memory allocated total",[32]="* 314 - Pi as `3..1415926535898`",[33]="* 315 - Presence of `-x` CLI argument when running Picotron headless",[34]="* 316 - Path specified when running headless",[35]="* 317 - `3.0` when a binary or html export, `1.0` when running on the BBS web player, `0.0` otherwise",[36]="* 318 - `1.0` when a html export (including BBS web player), `0.0` otherwise",[37]="* 320 - `1.0` when recording a gif, `0.0` otherwise",[38]="* 321 - amount of frames that the active gif capture has, `0.0` otherwise",[39]="* 322 - `1.0` when you're actively capturing a gif, `0.0` otherwise",[40]="* 330 - `1.0` when Picotron's battery saver is active, `0.0` otherwise",[41]="* 333 - +1 of the previous stat(333) every time.",[42]="* 400+c,0 - `1.0` when a note is held, `0.0` otherwise",[43]="* 400+c,1 - channel instrument",[44]="* 400+c,2 - channel volume",[45]="* 400+c,3 - channel pan",[46]="* 400+c,4 - channel pitch",[47]="* 400+c,5 - channel bend",[48]="* 400+c,6 - channel effect",[49]="* 400+c,7 - channel effect_p",[50]="* 400+c,8 - channel tick length",[51]="* 400+c,9 - channel row",[52]="* 400+c,10 - channel row tick",[53]="* 400+c,11 - channel sfx tick",[54]="* 400+c,12 - channel sfx index, `-1` if no sfx playing",[55]="* 400+c,13 - channel last played sfx index",[56]="* 400+c,19,addr - stereo output of buffer (returns number of samples)",[57]="* 400+c,20+n,addr - mono output of buffer for a node (0..7)",[58]="* 464 - bitfield indicating which channels are playing (sfx)",[59]="* 465, addr - copy last mixer stereo output buffer output is written as int16's to addr. (returns number of samples written)",[60]="* 466 - which pattern is playing (-1 when no music is playing)",[61]="* 467 - index of the left-most non-looping music channel",[62]="* 498 - bitfield of currently active audio channels system-wide",[63]="* 984 - Unknown",[64]="* 985 - Unknown",[65]="* 987 - miliseconds picotron has been running",[66]="* 988 - `1.0` if both left and right control keys are held, `0.0` otherwise",[67]=""},children={}}}}}},[3]={level=2,text="Returns",content={[1]="",[2]="The queried information from the stat, unique to `n`, see the valid stats above for information on specific stats.",[3]=""},children={}},[4]={level=2,text="Unknown stats",content={[1]="",[2]="More information on unknown stats.",[3]="",[4]="[stat.lua](https://github.com/akd-io/picotron/blob/main/drive/projects/stat/stat.lua) (a test from akd-io)",[5]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="308",content={[1]="",[2]="Observed value `1973.0` and `2334.0` in stat.lua output.",[3]="",[4]="No code references.",[5]=""},children={}},[2]={level=3,text="309",content={[1]="",[2]="Observed value `60531740.0` and `63912031.0` in stat.lua output.",[3]="",[4]="No code references.",[5]=""},children={}},[3]={level=3,text="310",content={[1]="",[2]="Observed value `551.0`, `565.0` and `3689.0` in stat.lua output.",[3]="",[4]="Same decompiled code as for stat(311) below, but passing 2 to pdisk_count_slots_by_kind() instead of 0.",[5]="",[6]="No code references.",[7]=""},children={}},[4]={level=3,text="311",content={[1]="",[2]="Functionality unknown.",[3]="",[4]="Observed value `15833.0`, `15819.0` and `12342.0` in stat.lua output.",[5]="",[6]="Decompiled code by Maxine:",[7]="",[8]="```C",[9]="if (stat_type != UNDOCUMENTED_311) goto LAB_00460c7f;",[10]="tmp_int0 = pdisk_count_slots_by_kind(0);",[11]="result_num = (lua_Number)tmp_int0;",[12]="```",[13]=""},children={[1]={level=4,text="Picotron Manual reference",content={[1]="Each process in Picotron has a limit of 32MB RAM, which includes both allocations for Lua objects, and data stored directly in RAM using memory functions like poke() and memcpy(). In the latter case, 4k pages are allocated when a page is written, and can not be deallocated during the process lifetime.",[2]="",[3]="Only 16MB of ram is addressable: 0x000000..0xffffff. Memory addresses below 0x80000 and above 0xf00000 are mostly reserved for system use, but anything in the 0x80000..0xefffff range can be safely used for arbitrary purposes.",[4]=""},children={}}}},[5]={level=3,text="313",content={[1]="",[2]="Amount of memory allocated",[3]="",[4]="Decompiled by Maxine",[5]="```C",[6]="    case UNDOCUMENTED_313:",[7]="        lua_pushnumber(L, cproc->mem_highwater);",[8]="        return 1;",[9]="```",[10]=""},children={}},[6]={level=3,text="984",content={[1]="",[2]="To do with yielding and coroutines",[3]="",[4]="Code reference: ",[5]="```lua",[6]="if costatus(c) == \"suspended\" and stat(984) == 0 then",[7]="```"},children={}},[7]={level=3,text="985",content={[1]="",[2]="Observed value `1.0` in stat.lua output.",[3]=""},children={}},[8]={level=3,text="988",content={[1]="",[2]="To do with minimal terminal setup",[3]="",[4]="Code reference:",[5]="```lua",[6]="-- give a guaranteed short window to skip",[7]="if (stat(988) > 0) bypass = true _signal(35)",[8]="```",[9]=""},children={}}}}}}}},[153]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/sspr/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="sspr(sprite,sx,sy,sw,sh,x,y,[dw],[dh][flip_x],[flip_y])",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="`sspr` draws a sprite, similar to [`spr()`](/functions/spr/main.md), but allows you to also resize the sprite.",[3]="",[4]="This is scaling sprites, e.g: zoom",[5]="",[6]="Source: [source.lua](source.lua)",[7]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="sprite: `index` or `userdata u8`",content={[1]="",[2]="What is drawn to the screen",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=3,text="sx,sy: `number`",content={[1]="",[2]="The coordinates of what is treated as the top left of `sprite`, allowing you to add offsets",[3]=""},children={}},[3]={level=3,text="sw,sh: `number`",content={[1]="",[2]="The width & height of `sprite` - allows you to crop the sprite.",[3]=""},children={}},[4]={level=3,text="dx,dy: `number`",content={[1]="",[2]="Where the sprite is to be drawn on screen",[3]=""},children={}},[5]={level=3,text="[dw],[dh]: `number`",content={[1]="",[2]="Optional, defaults to the proportions of the sprite",[3]="",[4]="The new width & height of `sprite`.",[5]="",[6]="`sprite` will be drawn rescaled to match the proportions of `[dw],[dh]`.",[7]=""},children={}},[6]={level=3,text="[flip_x],[flip_y]",content={[1]="",[2]="Flips the sprite on their respective axis",[3]=""},children={}}}},[3]={level=2,text="Examples",content={[1]="",[2]="```lua",[3]="local mapUD=userdata(\"u8\",480,270)",[4]="",[5]="set_draw_target(mapUD) --all graphical operations now go to the map userdata",[6]="map() --draw map",[7]="set_draw_target() --return graphical operations to the window/screen",[8]="",[9]="--mapwidth,mapheight is the proportions of the map",[10]="--x,y is where to draw the map",[11]="",[12]="sspr(mapUD,0,0,mapwidth,mapheight,x,y,mapwidth*2,mapheight*2)",[13]="```",[14]=""},children={}},[4]={level=2,text="Returns",content={[1]="",[2]="Unknown",[3]=""},children={}}}}}},[154]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/spr/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="spr(sprite,[x],[y],[flip_x],[flip_y])",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="`spr` draws a sprite from either a userdata or the spritebank",[3]="",[4]="This is scaling sprites, e.g: zoom",[5]="",[6]="Source: [source.lua](source.lua)",[7]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="sprite",content={[1]="",[2]="Sprite index from the spritesheet, or a `userdata u8` piece of data - this is what is drawn.",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=3,text="x,y",content={[1]="",[2]="The coordinates of where to draw the sprite",[3]=""},children={}},[3]={level=3,text="[flip_x],[flip_y]",content={[1]="",[2]="Flips the sprite on their respective axis",[3]=""},children={}}}},[3]={level=2,text="Examples",content={[1]="",[2]="Draw a sprite from sprite index 0 to position (0,0)",[3]="```lua",[4]="spr(0,0,0)",[5]="```",[6]="",[7]="Draw a sprite from a `userdata u8` to position (0,0)",[8]="```lua",[9]="sp=--[[pod_type=\"gfx\"]]unpod(\"b64:bHo0ALEAAAC4AQAA_gFweHUAQyBAQATw9jPwHDOwBQCvcDM3M-AUMzEzMAkACEuz8BSzBQCPcDNw8wxwM7AHAAGN8ABz_wxz8AQGADAAM-t3AAkFAO9wMzt-IPsMfjszMDM7fgkABo8_dz77BD53PgsAEz9_u34KABBf-gS7-gQIAAjvPxp_Nz67Pjd_PTMwMz0MAA6fcDN9frt_fTOwCAAEYPAAc7s_uwwBDwcAAFQM8wzwFAQAUPMM8P8D\")",[10]="",[11]="spr(sp,0,0)",[12]="```",[13]=""},children={}},[4]={level=2,text="Returns",content={[1]="",[2]="Unknown",[3]=""},children={}}}}}},[155]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/split/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="split(string,[seperator],[convert_numbers]): table",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="`split` converts the string `string` into a table, being seperated by the seperator `[seperator]`.",[3]="",[4]="Elements in the table are automatically converted into numbers if they are a number as a string (e.g: \"2\"); this can be disabled by settings `[convert_numbers]` to false.",[5]="",[6]="This can also be used in the form of `string:split([seperator],[convert_numbers])`.",[7]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`string`: string",content={[1]="",[2]="The string to split into a table.",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=3,text="`[seperator]`: string|number",content={[1]="",[2]="When seperator is a string, it splits the string each time it hits the seperator string.",[3]="",[4]="When seperator is a number, the string is split into n-character groups.",[5]=""},children={}},[3]={level=3,text="`[convert_numbers]`: boolean",content={[1]="",[2]="A truthy boolean of whether to convert number strings into numbers; defaults to true.",[3]=""},children={}}}},[3]={level=2,text="Returns",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`table`: table",content={[1]="",[2]="Returns the string split into a table.",[3]=""},children={}}}},[4]={level=2,text="Examples",content={[1]="",[2]="Sourced from the [Picotron Manual](https://www.lexaloffle.com/dl/docs/picotron_manual.html)",[3]="",[4]="```lua",[5]="?split(\"1,2,3,a,b\")   -- {1,2,3,\"a\",\"b\"}",[6]="?split(\"one:two:3\",\":\",false) -- {\"one\",\"two\",\"3\"}",[7]="?split(\"1,,2,\")   -- {1,\"\",2,\"\"}",[8]="```"},children={}}}}}},[156]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/socket/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="socket(addr): socket",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="`socket` creates and returns a socket (TCP or UDP) at address `addr`.",[3]="",[4]="Listener sockets can not be created while a process is sandboxed.",[5]="",[6]="i.e. BBS carts can proactively connect to a particular address; but can not receive connections from arbitrary sources.",[7]="",[8]="A socket with remote hosts writing (or connecting to) that port can then be accepted using `sock:accept()`.",[9]="",[10]="Sockets allow Picotron to send data to other machines (or local programs) that support the same type of socket;  currently TCP and UDP is supported.",[11]="",[12]="There is currently no web support, but web sockets will be added in the future.",[13]="",[14]="Sockets have [methods](methods/) for stuff like accepting and writing.",[15]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`addr`: string",content={[1]="",[2]="The address to create the socket on.",[3]="",[4]="A string consisting of the protocol (tcp:// or udp://), the ip address, followed by a port number \":1234\".",[5]="",[6]="IPV6 addresses should be enclosed in square brackets.",[7]="",[8]="To create a listener socket; you should use `[prot://]*:[port]`.",[9]=""},children={}}}},[3]={level=2,text="Returns",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`socket`: table|nil",content={[1]="",[2]="The created socket; nil if the socket failed to create.",[3]="",[4]="This has more [methods](methods/) for stuff like accepting and writing.",[5]="",[6]="There is also two properties shared with the socket:",[7]="",[8]="* `addr`: address",[9]="* `port`: port",[10]=""},children={}}}},[4]={level=2,text="Examples",content={[1]="",[2]="Sourced from the [Picotron Manual](https://www.lexaloffle.com/dl/docs/picotron_manual.html)",[3]="",[4]="```lua",[5]="sock = socket(\"tcp://example.com:80\")",[6]="sock:write(\"GET / HTTP/1.1\\\"r\\\"nHost: example.com\\\"r\\\"nConnection: close\\\"r\\\"n\\\"r\\\"n\")",[7]="?sock:read()",[8]="```",[9]="",[10]="A UDP socket that listens to any incoming traffic",[11]="```lua",[12]="sock = socket(\"udp://*:8899\")",[13]="```",[14]="",[15]="A server that receives messages from multiple clients",[16]="```lua",[17]="-- server (paste in terminal window)",[18]="listener = socket(\"tcp://*:8899\")",[19]="if (not listener) print(\"** could not create socket\")",[20]="local clients = {}",[21]="print(\"\")",[22]="while (listener) do",[23]="    print(\"\\\"rlistening \\\"f8\"..(\"-\\\"\\\"|/\")[1+(time()*4%4)\\\"1])",[24]="    local new_client = listener:accept()",[25]="    if (new_client) then ",[26]="        print(\"\\\"r\\\"fcnew client!\")",[27]="        print(\"addr: \"..new_client.addr)",[28]="        print(\"port: \"..new_client.port)",[29]="        print(\"\")",[30]="        add(clients, new_client)",[31]="    end",[32]="    for client in all(clients) do",[33]="        local dat = client:read()",[34]="        if (dat) print(\"\\\"rmessage: \"..dat) print\"\"",[35]="    end",[36]="end",[37]="```",[38]="",[39]="This is interacted with by opening another terminal window and connecting to the local server",[40]="```lua",[41]="s = socket(\"tcp://localhost:8899\")",[42]="s:write(\"hi!\")",[43]="```"},children={}}}}}},[157]={name="readme.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/socket/methods/readme.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="socket > methods",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="Sockets have special methods that can be used for interacting with the socket.",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Methods",content={[1]="",[2]="[accept](accept/main.md)"},children={}}}}}},[158]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/socket/methods/write/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="socket > methods > write(message)",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="Writes the string `message` to the socket.",[3]="",[4]="Returns the number of bytes written (which may be less than #str); or nil followed by an error message string.",[5]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Returns",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`bytes`: number|nil",content={[1]="",[2]="The amount of bytes written; nil when there is an error.",[3]=""},children={}}}},[3]={level=2,text="`error`: string|nil",content={[1]="",[2]="An error message that is returned if there is an error; nil otherwise."},children={}}}}}},[159]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/socket/methods/status/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="socket > methods > status()",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="Returns the status of the socket",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Returns",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`status`: string",content={[1]="",[2]="The status of the socket.",[3]="",[4]="One of the following:",[5]="",[6]="* \"ready\" - the socket is ready to read/write",[7]="* \"listening\" - the socket was created with a wildcard (*) address and ready to :accept() connections",[8]="* \"closed\" - :close() was called on the socket",[9]="* \"disconnected\" - the socket was closed by peer, or disconnected for some other reason",[10]="* \"invalid\" - observed when the socket is disconnected"},children={}}}}}}}},[160]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/socket/methods/read/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="socket > methods > read()",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="Read a string from a socket.",[3]="",[4]="This function is not blocking; it will return nothing when there is no data available on the socket.",[5]="",[6]="On error, returns nil followed by an error message.",[7]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Returns",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`message`: string|nil",content={[1]="",[2]="The string read from a socket; nil when there is an error or no message",[3]=""},children={}}}},[3]={level=2,text="`error`: string|nil",content={[1]="",[2]="An error message that is returned if there is an error; nil otherwise."},children={}}}}}},[161]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/socket/methods/close/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="socket > methods > close()",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="Close the socket if there is one."},children={}}}}}},[162]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/socket/methods/accept/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="socket > methods > accept()",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="This can be used with sockets that are listening to all traffic on a given port.",[3]="",[4]="When a new connection is made with TCP, or a UDP message is receieved from a new address+port, `:accept()` will return a new socket that can be used to communicate with that particular client, or `nil` if there are none waiting.",[5]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Returns",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`sock`: table|nil",content={[1]="",[2]="A new socket used to communicate with the accepted client; `nil` if there are no clients accepted.",[3]=""},children={}}}},[3]={level=2,text="Examples",content={[1]="",[2]="Sourced from the [Picotron Manual](https://www.lexaloffle.com/dl/docs/picotron_manual.html)",[3]="",[4]="```lua",[5]="sock2 = listener_socket:accept()",[6]="```"},children={}}}}}},[163]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/sfx/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="sfx(n, [channel], [offset], [length], [mix_volume])",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="Plays sound effect at index `n` (`0-64`) in channel `[channel]` (`0-15`) for `length` notes.",[3]="",[4]="The volume of the sound effect is `[mix_volume]`.",[5]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`n`: integer",content={[1]="",[2]="The sound effect index to play.",[3]="",[4]="If this is negative, it calls a special sfx command; these are as follows.",[5]=""},children={[1]={level=4,text="-1",content={[1]="",[2]="Stops playing any sfx on that channel.",[3]="",[4]="The existing channel state is not altered; stopping an sfx that uses an instrument with a long echo will not cut the echo short.",[5]=""},children={}},[2]={level=4,text="-2",content={[1]="",[2]="Stops playing any sfx on that channel, and also clears the channel state state: echos are cut short  and the channel is immediately silent.",[3]=""},children={}},[3]={level=4,text="-3",content={[1]="",[2]="Release any looping sfx on that channel",[3]=""},children={}},[4]={level=4,text="-4",content={[1]="",[2]="Set the mix volume.",[3]="",[4]="The channel mix volume is not reset until sfx() is called on that channel, so music channel mix volumes persist between calls to [`music()`](/picotron_api/functions/music/main.md).",[5]=""},children={}}}},[2]={level=3,text="`[channel]`: integer",content={[1]="",[2]="The designated channel to play the sound effect in; defaults to a free channel when `-1` or `nil` in one half of the audio channels.",[3]=""},children={}},[3]={level=3,text="`[offset]`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="The offset to play the sfx at; if this is negative, you can delay playback.",[3]=""},children={}},[4]={level=3,text="`[length]`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="The length of the notes to play.",[3]="",[4]="When the sfx is looping, length still means the number of (possibly repeated) notes to play.",[5]=""},children={}},[5]={level=3,text="`[mix_volume]`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="When `[mix_volume]` is given, the channel is mixed at that value (`0x40` means `1.0`).",[3]="",[4]="Otherwise, the value at `0x553a` is used (`0x40` by default).",[5]="",[6]="In addition to the per-channel mix volume, all  channels are subject to a per-process global volume specified at `0x5538` (default: `0x40` == `1.0`)."},children={}}}}}}}},[164]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/set_spr/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="set_spr(index, s, [flags_val])",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="`set_spr` adds or removes a sprite at a set index.",[3]="",[4]="Sprite flags are stored at 0xc000 (16k)",[5]="",[6]="Source: [source.lua](source.lua)",[7]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="index",content={[1]="",[2]="The index of the sprite you're modifying in the spritesheet.",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=3,text="s",content={[1]="",[2]="The sprite data to set in sprite `index`, form of a `userdata u8`.",[3]=""},children={}},[3]={level=3,text="[flags_val]",content={[1]="",[2]="The flag information to be set for sprite `index`, defaults to 0",[3]="",[4]="Poked into `0xc000`, offset by `index &= 0x3fff`",[5]=""},children={}}}},[3]={level=2,text="Returns",content={[1]="",[2]="This function does not return anything"},children={}}}}}},[165]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/set_draw_target/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="set_draw_target([ud])",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="Set the current draw target to userdata u8 `ud`; all draw operations go to this ",[3]="",[4]="When `ud` is not given; it defaults to the current display.",[5]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`[ud]`: userdata",content={[1]="",[2]="A 2d userdata u8"},children={}}}}}}}},[166]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/set_clipboard/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="set_clipboard(text)",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="`set_clipboard` sets the user's clipboard to `text`.",[3]="",[4]="For structured objects to be copied to the clipboard; use [pod()](/picotron_api/functions/pod/main.md) and [unpod()](/picotron_api/functions/unpod/main.md) with a plaintext format (`0x0` or `0x7`).",[5]="",[6]="This is typically used in conjunction with [get_clipboard()](/picotron_api/functions/get_clipboard/main.md).",[7]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`text`: string",content={[1]="",[2]="The text to be copied to the clipboard.",[3]=""},children={}}}},[3]={level=2,text="Examples",content={[1]="",[2]="Sourced from the [Picotron Manual](https://www.lexaloffle.com/dl/docs/picotron_manual.html)",[3]="",[4]="```lua",[5]="ud = userdata(\"u8\", 64, 64)",[6]="set_draw_target(ud)",[7]="circfill(32,32,31,8)",[8]="circfill(32,32,16,18)",[9]="set_draw_target()",[10]="set_clipboard(pod(ud, 0x7, {pod_type=\"image\"}))",[11]="--> can paste into the code editor",[12]="```",[13]="",[14]="```lua",[15]="out = \"[output]\\\"n\"",[16]="",[17]="function _update()",[18]="",[19]="    if key\"ctrl\" and keyp\"c\" then",[20]="        local test_str = \"test\"..flr(rnd(10000))",[21]="        set_clipboard(test_str)",[22]="        out ..= \"ctrl-c copied: \"..test_str..\"\\\"n\"",[23]="    end",[24]="",[25]="    if key\"ctrl\" and keyp\"v\" then",[26]="        out ..= \"ctrl-v pasted: \"..get_clipboard()..\"\\\"n\"",[27]="    end",[28]="",[29]="    -- this will only work for clipboard contents that is copied from within Picotron ",[30]="    -- (or within the same app when sandboxed), unless pasted with ctrl-v first.",[31]="    if key\"ctrl\" and keyp\"b\" then",[32]="        out ..= \"ctrl-b pasted: \"..get_clipboard()..\"\\\"n\"",[33]="    end",[34]="end",[35]="",[36]="function _draw()",[37]="    cls()",[38]="    print(out, 2,2,7)",[39]="end",[40]="```"},children={}}}}}},[167]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/send_message/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="send_message(proc_id, msg, [response])",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="`send_message` allows you to communicate with other processes.",[3]="",[4]="This is useful for communication between processes",[5]="",[6]="Source: [source.lua](source.lua)",[7]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="proc_id",content={[1]="",[2]="The process ID of which to send the message to",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=3,text="msg",content={[1]="",[2]="The message, a table, to send to the process `proc_id`.",[3]="",[4]="A message must have the key `event`, being a string that is sent to the process as its event.",[5]="",[6]="This can be any arbritrary string - handled by the process that receives it.",[7]=""},children={}},[3]={level=3,text="[response]",content={[1]="",[2]="Optional argument",[3]="",[4]="By setting `[response]` to `true`, it causes the function to stall the entire program until it receives a response from the other process.",[5]="",[6]="Note that blocking in this way can be quite slow - it is intended to be used in situations where it is acceptable to skip a frame or two while waiting for a reply.",[7]="",[8]="When `[response]` is a function, the event handler of the receiving process can reply with another message that is handled by that function.",[9]=""},children={}}}},[3]={level=2,text="Examples",content={[1]="",[2]="> From the Picotron Manual",[3]="",[4]="When reply is true, send_message blocks until the process responds with a reply. For example, paste the following in to terminal to set up a headless process that responds to \"get_id\" messages:",[5]="",[6]="```lua",[7]="store(\"/ram/get_id.lua\", [[",[8]="    id = 1",[9]="    function _update() end",[10]="    on_event(\"get_id\", function() ",[11]="        id += 1",[12]="        return {id = id} ",[13]="    end)",[14]="]])",[15]="pid2 = create_process(\"/ram/get_id.lua\")",[16]="```",[17]="",[18]="A unique id can be fetched from this process with:",[19]="",[20]="```lua",[21]="?send_message(pid2, {event = \"get_id\"}, true).id",[22]="```",[23]="",[24]="The true argument for `[response]` causes the message to be blocking - waiting for the response from `pid2`, being the data including the `id` it requests.",[25]=""},children={}},[4]={level=2,text="Sandboxed Nature",content={[1]="",[2]="Sandboxed processes can send messages to:",[3]="",[4]="* itself",[5]="* /system/",[6]="* wm",[7]="",[8]="alongside sending certain events:",[9]="",[10]="* \"set_palette\"",[11]="* \"broadcast\" *if* it has a contained event of `set_palette`",[12]=""},children={}},[5]={level=2,text="Returns",content={[1]="",[2]="This function returns nothing, unless you have a response, where it instead returns the response of the event."},children={}}}}}},[168]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/scoresub/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="scoresub(table_name,[score],[extra]): scores",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="Retrieve and optionally store scores in the online daatbase of Lexaloffle.",[3]="",[4]="This requires the user to be logged in and online (even if you aren't submitting any data); whether the user is logged in or not can be checked with [`stat(64)`](/picotron_api/functions/stat/main.md).",[5]="",[6]="When attempting to submit a score when there is no user logged in (not stat(64)) or the BBS is not reachable, scoresub returns nil. In the case where the network is temporarily unavailable (e.g. wifi is off on the host OS), the process will periodically re-attempt to submit the score.",[7]="",[8]="This is a non-blocking function and should be continuously polled to show live changes. Submitted scores are not returned until they have made the round trip to the server.",[9]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="Rate limiting",content={[1]="",[2]="`scoresub()` can be called frequently, but both incoming and outgoing changes to each table will update at most once per 2 seconds.",[3]="",[4]="The number of different channels that can be accessed by scoresub is also limited to 4 within 10 seconds. After that, it will return nil.",[5]=""},children={}},[2]={level=3,text="Table name spaces",content={[1]="",[2]="Each BBS cartridge has its own namespace for highscore tables, by internally prepending the bbs id to the table name. So for example, a BBS cart `ranger-0.p64` that is using a table `level1` is actually indexing that high score table as `ranger-level1` internally.",[3]="",[4]="This means that it is not possible for a BBS cartridge to conflict with another cart's high score table, or with local cartridges (which are further separated by prepending an underscore). It is unclear whether this means that local cartridges use `{cartname}_-{table}`, `_{table}` or something else.",[5]="",[6]="It has been recorded that if you have a persistent system, you are placed on different leaderboards than without a persistent system.",[7]="",[8]="You can do networking with scoresub, seen in @HowfWuff's [inuchat](https://www.Lexaloffle.com/bbs/?tid=156091).",[9]="",[10]="@HowfWuff has created a library for this, found [here](https://www.Lexaloffle.com/bbs/?tid=155740)",[11]=""},children={[1]={level=4,text="Format",content={[1]="BBS: `{bbs_id}-{table}`",[2]="Local: (Presumably, testing needed.) `_{table}` or `{basename}_-{table}`",[3]=""},children={}}}}}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`table_name`: string",content={[1]="",[2]="The name of the scoresub table, used as an identifier",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=3,text="`[score]`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="`[score]` is the new score for the user; each user only has one entry.",[3]=""},children={}},[3]={level=3,text="`[extra]`: string",content={[1]="",[2]="`[extra]` is a string (maximum 1024 characters, cropped to 1024 when over), this can be anything, e.g: puzzle solution, a message, or extra scoring data.",[3]=""},children={}}}},[3]={level=2,text="Returns",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`scores`: table",content={[1]="",[2]="A table of scores with up to 64 entries (the top 64).",[3]="",[4]="Each entry has the following fields:",[5]=""},children={[1]={level=4,text="`user_id`: string",content={[1]="",[2]="The Lexaloffle user id (unique to each user) of the player.",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=4,text="`username`: string",content={[1]="",[2]="The Lexaloffle username of the player (this may change, use `user_id` for a unique identifier)",[3]="",[4]="Each Lexaloffle username is 3-16 characters, containing only letters, numbers and underscores.",[5]=""},children={}},[3]={level=4,text="`score`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="The score of the player",[3]=""},children={}},[4]={level=4,text="`extra`: string",content={[1]="",[2]="A string, up to 1024 characters, that is attached to the entry",[3]=""},children={}},[5]={level=4,text="`icon`: userdata u8",content={[1]="",[2]="The user's Picotron icon (different to their Lexaloffle web icon; unique to Picotron).",[3]="16x16 using colours 0-32.",[4]=""},children={}}}}}}}}}},[169]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/rrectfill/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="rrectfill(x,y,width,height,radius,[col])",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="`rrect` draws a filled rounded rectangle with corners at `x`,`y` with a width of `width` and height of `height`. The radius of the rectangle is `radius`.",[3]="",[4]="When bit `0x800000000` in col is set, rrectfill draws inverted.",[5]="",[6]="For drawing normal filled rectangles, use [rectfill](/picotron_api/functions/rectfill/main.md).",[7]="",[8]="For drawing rounded rectangles (no filling, just outline), use [rrect](/picotron_api/functions/rrect/main.md).",[9]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`x`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="The x-coordinate of the rounded rectangle",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=3,text="`y`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="The y-coordinate of the rounded rectangle",[3]=""},children={}},[3]={level=3,text="`width`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="The width of the rounded rectangle; must be more than 0",[3]=""},children={}},[4]={level=3,text="`height`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="The height of the rounded rectangle; must be more than 0",[3]=""},children={}},[5]={level=3,text="`radius`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="The radius of the rounded rectangle; defaults to 0.",[3]="",[4]="The radius is clamped to `min(width,height)/2`",[5]=""},children={}},[6]={level=3,text="`[col]`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="The color of the rectangle; defaults to the last used color.",[3]=""},children={}}}},[3]={level=2,text="Examples",content={[1]="",[2]="Sourced from the [Picotron Manual](https://www.lexaloffle.com/dl/docs/picotron_manual.html)",[3]="",[4]="Draw a red (colour 8) rounded rectangle 40 pixels wide and 30 pixels talls with 3 pixels missing at each corner (radius 2):",[5]="```lua",[6]="rrectfill(100,50,40,30,2,8)",[7]="```"},children={}}}}}},[170]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/rrect/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="rrect(x,y,width,height,radius,[col])",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="`rrect` draws a rounded rectangle with corners at `x`,`y` with a width of `width` and height of `height`. The radius of the rectangle is `radius`.",[3]="",[4]="For drawing normal rectangles, use [rect](/picotron_api/functions/rect/main.md).",[5]="",[6]="For drawing filled rounded rectangles, use [rrectfill](/picotron_api/functions/rrectfill/main.md).",[7]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`x`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="The x-coordinate of the rounded rectangle",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=3,text="`y`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="The y-coordinate of the rounded rectangle",[3]=""},children={}},[3]={level=3,text="`width`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="The width of the rounded rectangle; must be more than 0",[3]=""},children={}},[4]={level=3,text="`height`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="The height of the rounded rectangle; must be more than 0",[3]=""},children={}},[5]={level=3,text="`radius`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="The radius of the rounded rectangle; defaults to 0.",[3]="",[4]="The radius is clamped to `min(width,height)/2`",[5]=""},children={}},[6]={level=3,text="`[col]`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="The color of the rectangle; defaults to the last used color."},children={}}}}}}}},[171]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/rm/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="rm(f0)",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="`rm` deletes `f0`, a file or directory (including all of the directory's contents).",[3]="",[4]="Attempting to delete `/desktop/host` just causes the Host OS to unmount, rather than deleting the folder and contents.",[5]="",[6]="Source: [source.lua](source.lua)",[7]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="f0",content={[1]="",[2]="The path to the file or directory to delete",[3]=""},children={}}}},[3]={level=2,text="Returns",content={[1]="",[2]="Returns something, unknown what it returns"},children={}}}}}},[172]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/rectfill/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="rectfill(x0,y0,x1,y1,[col])",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="`rectfill` draws a rectangle with corners at `x0`,`y0` and `x1`,`y1`.",[3]="",[4]="For drawing rectangles that are not filled (just an outline), use [rect](/picotron_api/functions/rect/main.md).",[5]="",[6]="When bit `0x800000000` in col is set, rectfill draws inverted.",[7]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`x0`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="The top left x-coordinate of the rectangle",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=3,text="`y0`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="The top left y-coordinate of the rectangle",[3]=""},children={}},[3]={level=3,text="`x1`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="The bottom right x-coordinate of the rectangle",[3]=""},children={}},[4]={level=3,text="`y1`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="The bottom right y-coordinate of the rectangle",[3]=""},children={}},[5]={level=3,text="`[col]`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="The color of the rectangle; defaults to the last used color."},children={}}}}}}}},[173]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/rect/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="rect(x0,y0,x1,y1,[col])",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="`rect` draws a rectangle with corners at `x0`,`y0` and `x1`,`y1`.",[3]="",[4]="For drawing filled rectangles, use [rectfill](/picotron_api/functions/rectfill/main.md).",[5]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`x0`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="The top left x-coordinate of the rectangle",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=3,text="`y0`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="The top left y-coordinate of the rectangle",[3]=""},children={}},[3]={level=3,text="`x1`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="The bottom right x-coordinate of the rectangle",[3]=""},children={}},[4]={level=3,text="`y1`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="The bottom right y-coordinate of the rectangle",[3]=""},children={}},[5]={level=3,text="`[col]`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="The color of the rectangle; defaults to the last used color."},children={}}}}}}}},[174]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/readtext/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="readtext([clear])",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="`readtext` is used to consume the next key pressed, this typically works in conjunction with [`peektext()`](/picotron_api/functions/peektext/main.md) and will return only a single character; being the next character in the list to be read.",[3]="",[4]="This is read from the *host* operating system's text entry system.",[5]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`[clear]`: boolean",content={[1]="",[2]="When `[clear]` is true; *all* remaining text in the queue is discarded.",[3]=""},children={}}}},[3]={level=2,text="Returns",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`textpresent`: boolean",content={[1]="",[2]="Whether there is text present to be consumed.",[3]=""},children={}}}},[4]={level=2,text="Example",content={[1]="",[2]="Sourced from the [Picotron Manual](https://www.lexaloffle.com/dl/docs/picotron_manual.html)",[3]="",[4]="```lua",[5]="while (peektext())",[6]="    c = readtext()",[7]="    printh(\"read text: \"..c)",[8]="end",[9]="```"},children={}}}}}},[175]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/pwd/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="pwd()",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="`pwd` returns the present working directory.",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Returns",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`directory`: string",content={[1]="",[2]="The present working directory"},children={}}}}}}}},[176]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/pset/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="pset(x,y,[col])",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="Sets the pixel at x, y to colour index col (0..63).",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`x`: number",content={[1]="The x position of the pixel",[2]=""},children={}},[2]={level=3,text="`y`: number",content={[1]="The y position of the pixel",[2]=""},children={}},[3]={level=3,text="`[col]`: number",content={[1]="Default: [current draw color](/picotron_api/functions/color/main.md)",[2]="",[3]="The color of the pixel",[4]=""},children={}}}},[3]={level=2,text="Examples",content={[1]="",[2]="```lua",[3]="for y=0,127 do",[4]="    for x=0,127 do",[5]="        pset(x, y, x*y/8)",[6]="    end",[7]="end",[8]="```"},children={}}}}}},[177]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/printh/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="printh(str)",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="printh() allows for you to print text to the Host OS Terminal.",[3]="",[4]="This allows for you to do more informative debugging of your program, alongside allowing stuff like logs in your program.",[5]="",[6]="[Setting up a Host OS terminal for Picotron](/guides/host_os_terminal/main.md)",[7]="",[8]="Source: [source.lua](source.lua)",[9]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="str",content={[1]="",[2]="String to print",[3]=""},children={}}}},[3]={level=2,text="Examples",content={[1]="",[2]="Prints the CPU usage every time the frame updates",[3]="```lua",[4]="function _update()",[5]="    printh(stat(1)*100 ..\"% cpu usage\")",[6]="end",[7]="```",[8]=""},children={}},[4]={level=2,text="Returns",content={[1]="",[2]="Unknown"},children={}}}}}},[178]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/print/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="print(str,x,y,color)",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="This prints text to the active display of Picotron.",[3]="",[4]="When there is no graphical display, e.g: Picotron is running headless (no display), or there is no window setup - this feeds to the [`printh`](/picotron_api/functions/printh/main.md) function.",[5]="",[6]="Source: [source.lua](source.lua)",[7]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="str",content={[1]="",[2]="String to print",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=3,text="x,y",content={[1]="",[2]="The top left position of the text that is printed",[3]=""},children={}},[3]={level=3,text="color",content={[1]="",[2]="The index of the color in the color palette to draw the text in.",[3]=""},children={}}}},[3]={level=2,text="Examples",content={[1]="",[2]="Prints the CPU usage in the top left of the screen in the color white (`7`)",[3]="",[4]="```lua",[5]="function _update()",[6]="    print(stat(1)*100 ..\"% cpu usage\",0,0,7)",[7]="end",[8]="```",[9]=""},children={}},[4]={level=2,text="Returns",content={[1]="",[2]="Returns the ending x & y coordinate of the text that is printed.",[3]="```lua",[4]="fx,fy=print(\"hi\")",[5]="```",[6]=""},children={}}}}}},[179]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/poke8/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="poke8(address,val)",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="`poke8` allows you to poke data `val` (8 bytes) into ram, starting at address `address`.",[3]="",[4]="This cannot be done between processes.",[5]="",[6]="To peek memory, you can use [peek()](/picotron_api/functions/peek/main.md).",[7]="",[8]="The generic poke function is [poke()](/picotron_api/functions/poke/main.md).",[9]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`address`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="The address to poke into.",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=3,text="`data`: integer",content={[1]="",[2]="The data to poke into the address and the following addresses."},children={}}}}}}}},[180]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/poke4/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="poke4(address,val)",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="`poke4` allows you to poke data `val` (4 bytes) into ram, starting at address `address`.",[3]="",[4]="This cannot be done between processes.",[5]="",[6]="To peek memory, you can use [peek()](/picotron_api/functions/peek/main.md).",[7]="",[8]="The generic poke function is [poke()](/picotron_api/functions/poke/main.md).",[9]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`address`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="The address to poke into.",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=3,text="`data`: integer",content={[1]="",[2]="The data to poke into the address and the following addresses."},children={}}}}}}}},[181]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/poke2/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="poke2(address,val)",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="`poke2` allows you to poke data `val` (2 bytes) into ram, starting at address `address`.",[3]="",[4]="This cannot be done between processes.",[5]="",[6]="To peek memory, you can use [peek()](/picotron_api/functions/peek/main.md).",[7]="",[8]="The generic poke function is [poke()](/picotron_api/functions/poke/main.md).",[9]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`address`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="The address to poke into.",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=3,text="`data`: integer",content={[1]="",[2]="The data to poke into the address and the following addresses."},children={}}}}}}}},[182]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/poke/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="poke(address,data,[...])",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="`poke` allows you to poke data into ram, starting at address `address`.",[3]="",[4]="This cannot be done between processes.",[5]="",[6]="To peek memory, you can use [peek()](/picotron_api/functions/peek/main.md).",[7]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`address`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="The address to poke into.",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=3,text="`data`: integer",content={[1]="",[2]="The data to poke into the address.",[3]=""},children={}},[3]={level=3,text="`[...]`: integer",content={[1]="",[2]="This can be continued to poke up to 65536 pieces of data into the consecutive 65536 addresses past `address`.",[3]="",[4]="They are identical to the `data` argument; being offset by +1."},children={}}}}}}}},[183]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/pod/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="pod(val,[flags],[metadata]): string",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="`pod` returns a binary string encoding the value `val`.",[3]="",[4]="Functions and other non-encodable values can be present in this input; but are replaced with nil and cannot be unpodded.",[5]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`val`: any",content={[1]="",[2]="The value to pod/encode.",[3]="",[4]="When `val` is a table which contains circular references, `pod()` returns nil.",[5]=""},children={}},[2]={level=3,text="`[flags]`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="> If `[flags]` is a table, it acts as `[metadata]`",[3]="",[4]="`[flags]` is a number, typically represented in hex, which controls how the output string is encoded; `0x00` is the default.",[5]=""},children={[1]={level=4,text="Available Flags:",content={[1]="",[2]="* 0x00 plaintext (& valid Lua)",[3]="* 0x01 pxu encode userdata in a compressed (RLE-style) form",[4]="* 0x02 lz4 binary compression pass (dictionary matching)",[5]="* 0x04 base64 text encoding (convert back into a text-friendly format)",[6]="* 0x08 (reserved for future use -- should be 0)",[7]="* 0x10 pxu option: store raw (smaller for already-compressed userdata)",[8]="* 0x20 strings option: store raw binary, instead of escaped plain-text",[9]="* 0x40 (reserved for future use -- should be 0)",[10]="* 0x80 pxu option: store in a stable rle format (used for undo stacks)",[11]="",[12]="",[13]="Plaintext (`0x00`) PODs can get quite large if they contain images or map data.",[14]="",[15]="A compressed binary encoding can be generated using flags `0x1` and `0x2`, which are normally used together as the pxu format aims to produce output that can be further compressed by lz4.",[16]="",[17]="[store()](/picotron_api/functions/store/main.md) and [send_message()](/picotron_api/functions/send_message/main.md) uses this format by default.",[18]="",[19]="",[20]="The flag `0x4` is used to encode a compressed string back into a plaintext format that can be used with the clipboard and is also used for things like embedded images in text (try copying and pasting from the sprite editor into the code editor).",[21]="",[22]="```lua",[23]="?pod({a=1,b=2}, 0x7)",[24]="unpod(\"b64:bHo0AAoAAAAJAAAAkHthPTEsYj0yfQ==\")",[25]="```",[26]="",[27]="Note that the output of `0x7` is still a legal lua snippet that returns the original value `{a=1,b=1}`.",[28]="In this case the resulting string is larger than raw encoding even though it is compressed, because of the overhead of bookkeeping information stored at each layer of encoding.",[29]=""},children={}}}},[3]={level=3,text="`[metadata]`: table",content={[1]="",[2]="When the second (`[flags]`) or third parameter is a table, it is taken to be `[metadata]`.",[3]="",[4]="This acts as metadata also encoded into the pod.",[5]=""},children={}}}},[3]={level=2,text="Returns",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`podded`: string",content={[1]="",[2]="The encoded string from value `val`.",[3]=""},children={}}}},[4]={level=2,text="Examples",content={[1]="",[2]="Sourced from the [Picotron Manual](https://www.lexaloffle.com/dl/docs/picotron_manual.html)",[3]="",[4]="```lua",[5]="?pod({a=1,b=2}) --{a=1,b=2}",[6]="```",[7]="",[8]="```lua",[9]="podded=pod(\"some text\", {author=\"alice\"}) -- --[[pod,author=\"alice\"]]\"some text\"",[10]="?podded",[11]="",[12]="value,meta=unpod(podded)",[13]="",[14]="?value --\"some text\"",[15]="?pod(meta) -- \"{author='alice'}\"",[16]="```",[17]="",[18]="```lua",[19]="?pod({a=1,b=2}, 0x7)",[20]="unpod(\"b64:bHo0AAoAAAAJAAAAkHthPTEsYj0yfQ==\")",[21]="```"},children={}}}}}},[184]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/pid/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="pid()",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="`pid` returns the process id of the process calling it.",[3]="",[4]="This is useful for communication between processes",[5]="",[6]="Source: [source.lua](source.lua)",[7]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Usage Example",content={[1]="",[2]="Using the filenav with the intention to save a
