file.",[3]="",[4]="```lua",[5]="create_process(\"/system/apps/filenav.p64\", {",[6]="    open_with = pid(), --shows the id of the process that opened it",[7]="    intention = \"save_file_as\",",[8]="    path=\"/desktop/\",",[9]="    use_ext=\"spr\",",[10]="    window_attribs = {",[11]="        workspace=\"current\",",[12]="        autoclose=true",[13]="    }",[14]="})",[15]="```",[16]=""},children={}},[3]={level=2,text="Returns",content={[1]="",[2]="The process ID"},children={}}}}}},[185]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/pget/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="pget(x,y)",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="Returns the color index at x,y.",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`x`: number",content={[1]="The x position of the pixel",[2]=""},children={}},[2]={level=3,text="`y`: number",content={[1]="The y position of the pixel",[2]=""},children={}}}},[3]={level=2,text="Returns",content={[1]="Color index at `x`,`y`.",[2]="",[3]="If the position is out of bounds, the color index will return as `0`.",[4]=""},children={}},[4]={level=2,text="Example",content={[1]="",[2]="```lua",[3]="while (true) do",[4]="    x, y = rnd(128), rnd(128)",[5]="    dx, dy = rnd(4)-2, rnd(4)-2",[6]="    pset(x, y, pget(dx+x, dy+y))",[7]="end",[8]="```"},children={}}}}}},[186]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/peektext/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="peektext()",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="`peektext` is used to see whether text has been entered; this will always return the same key until [`readtext()`](/picotron_api/functions/readtext/main.md) consumes it.",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Returns",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`textpresent`: boolean",content={[1]="",[2]="Whether there is text present to be consumed.",[3]=""},children={}}}},[3]={level=2,text="Example",content={[1]="",[2]="Sourced from the [Picotron Manual](https://www.lexaloffle.com/dl/docs/picotron_manual.html)",[3]="",[4]="```lua",[5]="while (peektext())",[6]="    c = readtext()",[7]="    printh(\"read text: \"..c)",[8]="end",[9]="```"},children={}}}}}},[187]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/peek8/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="peek8(address)",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="`peek8` peeks into the four memory addresses following `address`.",[3]="",[4]="This cannot be done between processes.",[5]="",[6]="The generic memory peek function is [peek()](/picotron_api/functions/peek/main.md).",[7]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`address`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="The address to peek from.",[3]=""},children={}}}},[3]={level=2,text="Returns",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`data`: integer",content={[1]="",[2]="The data in the addresses peeked."},children={}}}}}}}},[188]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/peek4/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="peek4(address)",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="`peek4` [peek()](/picotron_api/functions/peek/main.md).",[7]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`address`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="The address to peek from.",[3]=""},children={}}}},[3]={level=2,text="Returns",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`data`: integer",content={[1]="",[2]="The data in the addresses peeked."},children={}}}}}}}},[189]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/peek2/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="peek2(address)",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="`peek2` [peek()](/picotron_api/functions/peek/main.md).",[7]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`address`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="The address to peeked."},children={}}}}}}}},[190]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/peek/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="peek(address,[bytes])",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="`peek` allows you to peek into ram at address `address`.",[3]="",[4]="This can not be done between processes; you can technically do this with kernel functions through the use of [`_ppeek`](/picotron_api/functions/kernel/_ppeek/main.md).",[5]="",[6]="To poke memory, you can use [poke()](/picotron_api/functions/poke/main.md).",[7]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`address`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="The address to peek.",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=3,text="`[bytes]`: integer",content={[1]="",[2]="The amount of bytes to read; default 1, maximum 65536.",[3]=""},children={}}}},[3]={level=2,text="Returns",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`byte`: integer",content={[1]="",[2]="The byte at address `address`.",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=3,text="`[...]`: integer",content={[1]="",[2]="The consecutive bytes, the amount of bytes read is the `[bytes]` argument."},children={}}}},[4]={level=2,text="Examples",content={[1]="",[2]="Sourced from the [Picotron Manual](https://www.lexaloffle.com/dl/docs/picotron_manual.html)",[3]="",[4]="Read the first 2 bytes of video memory.",[5]="```lua",[6]="a, b = peek(0x10000, 2)",[7]="```"},children={}}}}}},[191]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/ovalfill/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="ovalfill(x0,y0,x1,y1,[col])",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="`oval` draws an oval that is symmetrical in x and y (an ellipse), with the given bounding rectangle.",[3]="",[4]="For drawing circles, use [circfill](/picotron_api/functions/circfill/main.md).",[5]="",[6]="When bit `0x800000000` in col is set, ovalfill is drawn inverted.",[7]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`x0`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="The top left x-coordinate of the oval",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=3,text="`y0`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="The top left y-coordinate of the oval",[3]=""},children={}},[3]={level=3,text="`x1`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="The bottom right x-coordinate of the oval",[3]=""},children={}},[4]={level=3,text="`y1`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="The bottom right y-coordinate of the oval",[3]=""},children={}},[5]={level=3,text="`[col]`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="The color of the oval; defaults to the last used color."},children={}}}}}}}},[192]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/oval/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="oval(x0,y0,x1,y1,[col])",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="`oval` draws an oval that is symmetrical in x and y (an ellipse), with the given bounding rectangle.",[3]="",[4]="For drawing circles, use [circ](/picotron_api/functions/circ/main.md).",[5]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`x0`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="The top left x-coordinate of the oval",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=3,text="`y0`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="The top left y-coordinate of the oval",[3]=""},children={}},[3]={level=3,text="`x1`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="The bottom right x-coordinate of the oval",[3]=""},children={}},[4]={level=3,text="`y1`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="The bottom right y-coordinate of the oval",[3]=""},children={}},[5]={level=3,text="`[col]`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="The color of the oval; defaults to the last used color."},children={}}}}}}}},[193]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/open/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="open(location)",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="Opens the file location `location` using the [`open` utility](/system/details/util/open/main.md).",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`location`: string",content={[1]="",[2]="The filepath to open; relative to the present working directory",[3]=""},children={}}}},[3]={level=2,text="Examples",content={[1]="",[2]="Sourced from the [Picotron Manual](https://www.lexaloffle.com/dl/docs/picotron_manual.html)",[3]="",[4]="Open a `readme.txt` stored in the working directory",[5]="```lua",[6]="open(\"readme.txt\")",[7]="```"},children={}}}}}},[194]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/on_event/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="on_event(event, function)",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="`on_event` allows for you to subscribe to the event `event` and act with function `function` upon the event.",[3]="",[4]="You can subscribe to the same event multiple times.",[5]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`event`: string",content={[1]="",[2]="The event to listen to, this can be any arbritrary value, but there are some default events as listed below.",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=3,text="`function`: function|nil",content={[1]="",[2]="This function is called upon the event, when `nil` it removes all subscriptions to the event.",[3]="It is called with a `msg` table, consisting of data to do with the event. Detailed event information is found [here](/picotron_api/events/readme.md)"},children={}}}}}}}},[195]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/note/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="note([pitch], [inst], [vol], [effect], [effect_p], [channel], [retrig], [panning])",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="This provides low level control over the state of a channel.",[3]="",[4]="It is useful in more niche situations, like audio authoring tools and size-coding.",[5]="",[6]="Internally this is what is used to play each row of a sfx when one is active. Use `0xff` to indicate an attribute should not be altered.",[7]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`[pitch]`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="Channel pitch; default `48` (middle C)",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=3,text="`[inst]`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="Instrument index; default `0`",[3]=""},children={}},[3]={level=3,text="`[vol]`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="Volume of the note; default `64`",[3]=""},children={}},[4]={level=3,text="`[effectchannel]`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="Effect; default `0`",[3]=""},children={}},[5]={level=3,text="`[effect_p]`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="Effect parameter; default `0`",[3]=""},children={}},[6]={level=3,text="`[channel]`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="Channel index (`0..15`); default `0`",[3]=""},children={}},[7]={level=3,text="`[retrig]`: boolean",content={[1]="",[2]="Force retrigger; default `false`",[3]=""},children={}},[8]={level=3,text="`[panning]`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="Set channel panning (`-128..127`)",[3]=""},children={}}}}}},[2]={level=1,text="note()",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="Kill all channels (including leftover echo and decay envelopes).",[3]="",[4]="This is equivalent to",[5]="```lua",[6]="sfx(-2,-1)",[7]="```"},children={}}}}}},[196]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/mv/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="mv(src_p, dest_p)",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="`mv` moves the file or folder from `src_p` to `dest_p`.",[3]="",[4]="Source: [source.lua](source.lua)",[5]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={}},[3]={level=2,text="src_p",content={[1]="",[2]="The path to the source file/folder",[3]=""},children={}},[4]={level=2,text="dest_p",content={[1]="",[2]="Path to a folder to put the source file/folder into",[3]=""},children={}},[5]={level=2,text="Returns",content={[1]="",[2]="If src is nil > \"could not resolve source path\"",[3]="If dest is nil > \"could not resolve destination path\"",[4]="If using a protocol (e.g: `bbs://`) > \"can not write to {protocol you attempted}://\"",[5]="",[6]="More returns are for other failures, unknown.",[7]=""},children={}},[6]={level=2,text="Future",content={[1]="",[2]="Ability to write to protocols"},children={}}}}}},[197]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/music/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="music(n, [fade_len], [channel_mask], [base_addr], [tick_offset])",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="Play music starting from pattern `n`.",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`n`: integer",content={[1]="",[2]="The pattern index to play.",[3]="",[4]="If this is `-1`; it stops music from playing.",[5]=""},children={}},[2]={level=3,text="`[fade_len]`: integer",content={[1]="",[2]="`[fade_len]` is the time (in ms) to fade over a pattern.",[3]=""},children={}},[3]={level=3,text="`[channel_mask]`: bitfield",content={[1]="",[2]="`[channel_mask]` is a bitfield that specifies which channels reserve for music only; low bits first.",[3]=""},children={}},[4]={level=3,text="`[base_addr]`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="When `[base_addr]` is given, the channels used to play music are assigned that location in memory to read data from.",[3]="",[4]="This can be used to load multiple `.sfx` files into memory and play them at the same time.",[5]=""},children={}},[5]={level=3,text="`[tick_offset]`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="An optional offset to start playing from.",[3]="",[4]="This is given in ticks rather than rows as it supports patterns with tricks that play back at different speeds.",[5]=""},children={}}}},[3]={level=2,text="Examples",content={[1]="",[2]="Sourced from the [Picotron Manual](https://www.lexaloffle.com/dl/docs/picotron_manual.html)",[3]="",[4]="Play only on the first three channels 0..2, the lowest three bits should be set:",[5]="```lua",[6]="music(0, nil, 0x7) -- bits: 0x1 | 0x2 | 0x4",[7]="```",[8]="",[9]="Load some music at `0x80000` and play it without interfering with sound effects stored at the default location of `0x30000`",[10]="```lua",[11]="fetch(\"sfx/title.sfx\"):poke(0x80000) -- load 256k into 0x80000..0xbffff",[12]="music(0, nil, nil, 0x80000) -- play music using 0x80000 as the audio base address",[13]="```",[14]="",[15]="Start from row 2 of a track that has spd 16, use",[16]="```lua",[17]="music(0, nil, nil, nil, 2 * 16)",[18]="```"},children={}}}}}},[198]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/mset/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="mset(x,y,tile)",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="Sets the tile at `x`,`y` to tile `tile`.",[3]="",[4]="This is equivalent to userdata's [`:set`](/picotron_api/userdata/methods/set/main.md)",[5]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`x`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="The x coordinate of the tile to set",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=3,text="`y`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="The y coordinate of the tile to set",[3]=""},children={}},[3]={level=3,text="`tile`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="The sprite index to set at tile `x`,`y`",[3]=""},children={}}}},[3]={level=2,text="Returns",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`tile`: integer",content={[1]="",[2]="The tile at `x`,`y`.",[3]=""},children={}}}},[4]={level=2,text="Examples",content={[1]="",[2]="Sourced from the [Picotron Manual](https://www.lexaloffle.com/dl/docs/picotron_manual.html)",[3]="",[4]="```lua",[5]="mymap = userdata(\"i16\", 32,32)",[6]="-- using standard userdata get and set:",[7]="mymap:set(1,3,42)",[8]="?mymap:get(1,3) -- 42",[9]="-- alternatively:",[10]="memmap(mymap, 0x100000) -- set mymap as the current working map",[11]="mset(1,3,117)",[12]="?mget(1,3) -- 117",[13]="```"},children={}}}}}},[199]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/mouselock/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="mouselock(lock, event_sensitivity, move_sensitivity)",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="`mouselock` is used to make a request to the host operating system's window manager to capture the mouse, allowing for it to control sensitivity and movement speed.",[3]="",[4]="This returns the relative position since the last frame",[5]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`lock`: boolean",content={[1]="",[2]="Whether to lock the mouse or not",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=3,text="`event_sensitivity`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="A number between 0 and 4 that determines how fast dx,dy changes (1.0 == 1/picotron pixel)",[3]=""},children={}},[3]={level=3,text="`move_sensitivity`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="A number between 0 and 4 that determines the mouse sensitivity (1.0 == normal speed)",[3]=""},children={}}}},[3]={level=2,text="Returns",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`dx`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="The distance on the x-axis that the mouse pointer has moved",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=3,text="`dy`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="The distance on the y-axis that the mouse pointer has moved",[3]=""},children={}}}},[4]={level=2,text="Example",content={[1]="",[2]="Sourced from the [Picotron Manual](https://www.lexaloffle.com/dl/docs/picotron_manual.html)",[3]="",[4]="```lua",[5]="local size, col = 20, 16",[6]="function _draw()",[7]="    cls()",[8]="    circfill(240, 135, size*4, col)",[9]="    local _,_,mb = mouse()",[10]="    dx,dy = mouselock(mb > 0, 0.05, 0) -- dx,dy change slowly, stop mouse moving",[11]="    size += dx  --  left,right to control size",[12]="    col  += dy  --  up,down to control colour",[13]="end",[14]="```"},children={}}}}}},[200]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/mouse/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="mouse([new_mx,new_my])",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="`mouse` is used to get and set the positioning of the cursor relative to the window.",[3]="",[4]="Source: [source.lua](source.lua)",[5]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="new_mx",content={[1]="",[2]="The new x position of the mouse",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=3,text="new_my",content={[1]="",[2]="The new y position of the mouse",[3]=""},children={}}}},[3]={level=2,text="Returns",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="mouse_x",content={[1]="",[2]="x-coordinate of the mouse",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=3,text="mouse_y",content={[1]="",[2]="y-coordinate of the mouse",[3]=""},children={}},[3]={level=3,text="mouse_b",content={[1]="",[2]="a bitfield:",[3]="",[4]="* 0x1 left mouse button",[5]="* 0x2 right mouse button",[6]="* 0x4 middle mouse button",[7]=" "},children={}},[4]={level=3,text="wheel_x",content={[1]="",[2]="The amount scrolled on the scrollwheel in the x-axis",[3]=""},children={}},[5]={level=3,text="wheel_y",content={[1]="",[2]="The amount scrolled on the scrollwheel in the y-axis",[3]=""},children={}}}},[4]={level=2,text="Examples",content={[1]="",[2]="Get the properties of the mouse & print when you left click or right click.",[3]="```lua",[4]="function _update()",[5]=" mouse_x, mouse_y, mouse_b, wheel_x, wheel_y = mouse()",[6]=" if (mouse_b&0x1==0x1) then",[7]="  print(\"left click!\")",[8]=" end",[9]=" if (mouse_b&0x2==0x2) then",[10]="  print(\"right click!\")",[11]=" end",[12]="end",[13]="```",[14]=""},children={}},[5]={level=2,text="Limitations",content={[1]="",[2]="The mouse() function does not allow you to set full properties of the mouse.",[3]="",[4]="This can be fixed using the following snippet to have access to the full mouse properties.",[5]="",[6]="```lua",[7]="--@astralsparv",[8]="function setMouse(x,y,b)",[9]="    send_message(3,{event=\"mouse\",mx=x,my=y,mb=b or 0})",[10]="end",[11]="```",[12]=""},children={}}}}}},[201]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/mkdir/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="mkdir(p)",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="`mkdir` creates a new directory (empty folder) at the path `p`",[3]="",[4]="This does not support protocols (e.g: bbs://)",[5]="",[6]="Source: [source.lua](source.lua)",[7]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="p",content={[1]="",[2]="The path for the new directory",[3]=""},children={}}}},[3]={level=2,text="Returns",content={[1]="",[2]="This function returns an error in the form of a string if the function fails.",[3]="Otherwise, returns nil",[4]=""},children={}},[4]={level=2,text="Future",content={[1]="",[2]="Ability to write to protocols"},children={}}}}}},[202]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/mget/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="mget(x,y): integer",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="Returns the tile in the currently loaded map at `x`,`y`.",[3]="",[4]="This is equivalent to userdata's [`:get`](/picotron_api/userdata/methods/get/main.md)",[5]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`x`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="The x coordinate of the tile to fetch",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=3,text="`y`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="The y coordinate of the tile to fetch",[3]=""},children={}}}},[3]={level=2,text="Returns",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`tile`: integer",content={[1]="",[2]="The tile at `x`,`y`.",[3]=""},children={}}}},[4]={level=2,text="Examples",content={[1]="",[2]="Sourced from the [Picotron Manual](https://www.lexaloffle.com/dl/docs/picotron_manual.html)",[3]="",[4]="```lua",[5]="mymap = userdata(\"i16\", 32,32)",[6]="-- using standard userdata get and set:",[7]="mymap:set(1,3,42)",[8]="?mymap:get(1,3) -- 42",[9]="-- alternatively:",[10]="memmap(mymap, 0x100000) -- set mymap as the current working map",[11]="mset(1,3,117)",[12]="?mget(1,3) -- 117",[13]="```"},children={}}}}}},[203]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/menuitem/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="menuitem",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="`menuitem` lets you edit the drop down menu on the window / context menu of the window.",[3]="",[4]="This has multiple different argument setups.",[5]="",[6]="Shortcuts in the form \"CTRL-A\"..\"CTRL-Z\" and \"CTRL-0\"..\"CTRL-9\" apply automatically",[7]=""},children={}}}},[2]={level=1,text="menuitem()",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="Leaving the function with no arguments clears the menu of its items",[3]=""},children={}}}},[3]={level=1,text="menuitem(m)",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="Picotron method for menu items, more capability than the legacy support p8 method",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`m`: string/table",content={[1]="",[2]="Setting this to `---` creates a divider menu item",[3]="",[4]="Otherwise modifies the menu through `m` acting as a table.",[5]="",[6]="`m` having the following values can have different effects:",[7]=""},children={[1]={level=4,text="`id`: string",content={[1]="",[2]="Sets the ID of the item, replaces any item with this id with this.",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=4,text="`label`: string",content={[1]="",[2]="The text to display as the option",[3]="",[4]="A lack of label causes the item to be deleted",[5]=""},children={}},[3]={level=4,text="`action`: function",content={[1]="",[2]="A function to call when you select this option.",[3]=""},children={}}}}}},[3]={level=2,text="Examples",content={[1]="",[2]="The following example program uses a menu to control the draw colour:",[3]="```lua",[4]="col = 0",[5]="window{width=160, height=120} -- comment for fullscreen example",[6]="menuitem{",[7]="    id = \"col_changer\",",[8]="    label = \"Next Colour\",",[9]="    shortcut = \"CTRL-N\",",[10]="    action = function()",[11]="        col = (col + 1) % 10 -- cycle colour",[12]="    end",[13]="}",[14]="",[15]="function _draw()",[16]="    cls(8 + col)",[17]="end",[18]="```"},children={}}}}}},[204]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/memset/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="memset(dest_addr,val,length)",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="Write a byte (8-bit number) `val` into address `dest_addr` and the consecutive `length` amount of bytes.",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`dest_addr`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="The address to set byte `val` at",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=3,text="`val`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="A byte (8-bit number) to put in `dest_addr` and the consecutive addresses",[3]=""},children={}},[3]={level=3,text="`length`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="The amount of bytes to set; occur consecutively after `dest_addr`",[3]=""},children={}}}},[3]={level=2,text="Examples",content={[1]="",[2]="Sourced from the [Picotron Manual](https://www.lexaloffle.com/dl/docs/picotron_manual.html)",[3]="",[4]="Fill half of video memory with 0xc8",[5]="```lua",[6]="> memset(0x10000, 0xc8, 0x10000)",[7]="```"},children={}}}}}},[205]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/memmap/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="memmap(ud,addr,[offset],[len])",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="`memmap` allows you to write a 4k (`0x1000`) userdata, `ud` to an address, `addr` in memory",[3]="",[4]="Source: [source.lua](source.lua)",[5]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="ud",content={[1]="",[2]="The userdata that is mapped into memory",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=3,text="addr",content={[1]="",[2]="The address to map the userdata into",[3]="",[4]="Mapping to address `0x100000` automatically unmaps the loaded map data & replacing the loaded map in memory with `ud`.",[5]=""},children={}},[3]={level=3,text="[offset]",content={[1]="",[2]="Unknown",[3]="",[4]="> Presuming that it's the offset in either memory in the userdata",[5]=""},children={}},[4]={level=3,text="[len]",content={[1]="",[2]="Unknown",[3]="",[4]="> Presuming that it's the length of the userdata"},children={}}}},[3]={level=2,text="Returns",content={[1]="",[2]="Returns the inputted `ud`",[3]="",[4]="Allows things such as:",[5]="",[6]="```",[7]="pfxdat = fetch(\"tune.sfx\"):memmap(0x30000)",[8]="```"},children={}}}}}},[206]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/memcpy/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="memcpy(dest_addr,source_addr,length)",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="Copy `length` amount of bytes of base ram from address `source_addr` to address `dest_addr`.",[3]="",[4]="Sections can be overlapping; but causes the function to be slower",[5]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`dest_addr`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="The address to copy memory to",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=3,text="`source_addr`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="The address to copy memory from",[3]=""},children={}},[3]={level=3,text="`length`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="The amount of bytes to copy"},children={}}}}}}}},[207]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/max/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="max(a,b): max",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="Returns the bigger of two numbers. ",[2]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`a`: number",content={[1]="A number to be compared with b",[2]=""},children={}},[2]={level=3,text="`b`: number",content={[1]="A number to be compared with a",[2]=""},children={}}}},[3]={level=2,text="Returns",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`max`: number",content={[1]="The bigger of the two numbers",[2]=""},children={}}}},[4]={level=2,text="Examples",content={[1]="",[2]="Make sure a value doesn't go below zero, useful for healthbars and bounding boxes.",[3]="```lua",[4]="player.hp = max( 0, player.hp-1 )",[5]="```",[6]=""},children={}}}}}},[208]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/map/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="map(ud, [b], [...])",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="`map` draws a map, either through picotron system or legacy pico-8 method",[3]="",[4]="Source: [source.lua](source.lua)",[5]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Picotron System",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="ud",content={[1]="",[2]="The map data in the datatype `userdata`",[3]="",[4]="If `ud` is not in the datatype `userdata`, it follows the legacy Pico-8 format.",[5]=""},children={}},[2]={level=3,text="[b]",content={[1]="",[2]="Unknown",[3]=""},children={}},[3]={level=3,text="[...]",content={[1]="",[2]="Extra arguments that are sent through to the system _draw_map function.",[3]=""},children={}},[4]={level=3,text="> Returns",content={[1]="",[2]="This function does not return anything",[3]=""},children={}}}},[3]={level=2,text="Legacy Pico-8 System",content={[1]="",[2]="The legacy Pico-8 system is added for legacy support and an alternative way to use maps.",[3]="",[4]="Legacy Pico-8 function",[5]="",[6]="`map(celx, cely, sx, sy, celw, celh, [layer])`",[7]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="celx",content={[1]="",[2]="The column location of the map cell in the upper left corner of the region to draw, where 0 is the leftmost column.",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=3,text="cely",content={[1]="",[2]="The row location of the map cell in the upper left corner of the region to draw, where 0 is the topmost row.",[3]=""},children={}},[3]={level=3,text="sx",content={[1]="",[2]="The x coordinate of the screen to place the upper left corner.",[3]="",[4]=""},children={}},[4]={level=3,text="sy",content={[1]="",[2]="The y coordinate of the screen to place the upper left corner.",[3]=""},children={}},[5]={level=3,text="celw",content={[1]="",[2]="The number of map cells wide in the region to draw.",[3]=""},children={}},[6]={level=3,text="celh",content={[1]="",[2]="The number of map cells tall in the region to draw.",[3]=""},children={}},[7]={level=3,text="[layer]",content={[1]="",[2]="If specified, only draw sprites that have flags set for every bit in this value (a bitfield). The default is 0 (draw all sprites).",[3]=""},children={}}}},[4]={level=2,text="Returns",content={[1]="",[2]="This function does not return anything"},children={}}}}}},[209]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/ls/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="ls([directory])",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="`ls` returns the files and folders in the given path relative to the current working directory.",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`[directory]`: string|nil",content={[1]="",[2]="The relative path to list the files and folders of.",[3]="",[4]="If this is blank; this lists the files and folders of the current working directory.",[5]="",[6]="If this starts with `/`; it is an exact path and the current working directory plays no effect.",[7]=""},children={}}}},[3]={level=2,text="Returns",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`list`: table",content={[1]="",[2]="A table of the files and folders in the directory; this is relative to the directory and will not be the fullpath."},children={}}}}}}}},[210]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/line/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="line(x0,y0,[x1,y1,[col]])",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="`line` draws a line from `x0`,`y0` to `x1`,`y1`.",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`x0`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="The first x-coordinate of the line.",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=3,text="`y0`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="The first y-coordinate of the line.",[3]=""},children={}},[3]={level=3,text="`x1`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="The second x-coordinate of the line; defaults to the end of the last drawn line.",[3]=""},children={}},[4]={level=3,text="`y1`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="The second y-coordinate of the line; defaults to the end of the last drawn line.",[3]=""},children={}},[5]={level=3,text="`[col]`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="The color of the line; defaults to the last used color.",[3]=""},children={}}}}}},[2]={level=1,text="line()",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="line() with no parameters means that the next call to line(x1, y1) will only set the end points without drawing.",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Examples",content={[1]="",[2]="```lua",[3]="function _draw()",[4]="    cls()",[5]="    line()",[6]="    for i=0,6 do",[7]="        line(64+cos(t()+i/6)*20, 64+sin(t()+i/6)*20, 8+i)",[8]="    end",[9]="end",[10]="```"},children={}}}}}},[211]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/keyp/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="keyp(k,[raw])",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="`keyp` queries wheter a keyboard key is pressed.",[3]="",[4]="By default, `keyp()` uses the local keyboard layout; On an AZERTY keyboard, `keyp(\"a\")` is true when the key to the right of `Tab` is pressed.",[5]="",[6]="You can use the raw layout by setting `[raw]` to true and using `k` as a raw scancode.",[7]="",[8]="To get the raw layout, use true as the second parameter to indicate that `k` should be the name of the raw scancode.",[9]="",[10]="This is typically used in menus or grid-based player movement.",[11]="",[12]="The state that keyp() reads is reset at the start of each call to [_update()](/picotron_api/program_structure/main.md), so it is preferable to use keyp only from inside that call and not from [_draw()](/picotron_api/program_structure/main.md), which might be called less frequently.",[13]="",[14]="For keyboard state, use [key](/picotron_api/functions/key/main.md).",[15]="",[16]="For controller button presses, use [btnp](/picotron_api/functions/btn/main.md).",[17]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`k`: string",content={[1]="",[2]="The name of each key is the same as the character it produces on a US keyboard with some exceptions: \"space\", \"delete\", \"enter\", \"tab\", \"ctrl\", \"shift\", \"alt\", \"pageup\", \"pagedown\".",[3]=""},children={[1]={level=4,text="When `[raw]` is not true",content={[1]="",[2]="The key to query whether it's pressed or not.",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=4,text="When `[raw]` is true",content={[1]="",[2]="The raw scancode whether it's pressed or not.",[3]=""},children={}}}},[2]={level=3,text="`[raw]`: boolean",content={[1]="",[2]="Whether or not `k` is a raw scancode.",[3]=""},children={}}}},[3]={level=2,text="Returns",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`pressed`: boolean",content={[1]="",[2]="Returns `true` when the requested key is pressed, `false` otherwise.",[3]=""},children={}}}},[4]={level=2,text="Examples",content={[1]="",[2]="Sourced from the [Picotron Manual](https://www.lexaloffle.com/dl/docs/picotron_manual.html)",[3]="",[4]="Whether the key to the right of `CAPSLOCK` is pressed, regardless of local keyboard layout.",[5]="```lua",[6]="?keyp(\"a\", true)",[7]="```",[8]="",[9]="Keybind example",[10]="```lua",[11]="if (key\"ctrl\" and keyp\"a\") printh(\"CTRL-A Pressed\")",[12]="```",[13]=""},children={}}}},[2]={level=1,text="keyp()",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="Returns true when any key is pressed.",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Returns",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`down`: boolean",content={[1]="",[2]="Returns `true` when any key is pressed, `false` otherwise."},children={}}}}}}}},[212]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/key/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="key(k,[raw])",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="`key` queries the key state of a keyboard key.",[3]="",[4]="By default, `key()` uses the local keyboard layout; On an AZERTY keyboard, `key(\"a\")` is true when the key to the right of `Tab` is pressed.",[5]="",[6]="You can use the raw layout by setting `[raw]` to true and using `k` as a raw scancode.",[7]="",[8]="To get the raw layout, use true as the second parameter to indicate that `k` should be the name of the raw scancode.",[9]="",[10]="For keyboard key presses, use [keyp](/picotron_api/functions/keyp/main.md).",[11]="",[12]="For controller inputs, use [btn](/picotron_api/functions/btn/main.md).",[13]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`k`: string",content={[1]="",[2]="The name of each key is the same as the character it produces on a US keyboard with some exceptions: \"space\", \"delete\", \"enter\", \"tab\", \"ctrl\", \"shift\", \"alt\", \"pageup\", \"pagedown\".",[3]=""},children={[1]={level=4,text="When `[raw]` is not true",content={[1]="",[2]="The key to query the state of.",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=4,text="When `[raw]` is true",content={[1]="",[2]="The raw scancode to query the state of",[3]=""},children={}}}},[2]={level=3,text="`[raw]`: boolean",content={[1]="",[2]="Whether or not `k` is a raw scancode.",[3]=""},children={}}}},[3]={level=2,text="Returns",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`down`: boolean",content={[1]="",[2]="Returns `true` when the requested key is down, `false` otherwise.",[3]=""},children={}}}},[4]={level=2,text="Examples",content={[1]="",[2]="Sourced from the [Picotron Manual](https://www.lexaloffle.com/dl/docs/picotron_manual.html)",[3]="",[4]="Whether the key to the right of `CAPSLOCK` is held, regardless of local keyboard layout.",[5]="```lua",[6]="?key(\"a\", true)",[7]="```",[8]="",[9]="Keybind example",[10]="```lua",[11]="if (key\"ctrl\" and keyp\"a\") printh(\"CTRL-A Pressed\")",[12]="```",[13]="",[14]="```lua",[15]="function _draw()",[16]="    cls(1)",[17]="    -- draw when either shift key is held down",[18]="    if (key(\"shift\")) circfill(100,100,40,12)",[19]="end",[20]="```",[21]=""},children={}}}},[2]={level=1,text="key()",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="Returns true when any key is down.",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Returns",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`down`: boolean",content={[1]="",[2]="Returns `true` when any key is down, `false` otherwise."},children={}}}}}}}},[213]={name="readme.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/kernel/readme.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="Picotron API > Functions > Kernel Functions",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="Kernel functions are special functions allocated for boot.lua, the window manager and the process manager.",[3]="",[4]="These are disabled for cartridges through the use of `/system/lib/jettison.lua`",[5]="",[6]="This can be re-enabled through the use of the following snippet ***first thing in the program***; this will temporarily disable `jettison.lua` to re-run itself, then enabling `jettison.lua` and exiting the program.",[7]="",[8]="This snippet is sourced from @Soupster (found in the Picotron Discord server).",[9]="",[10]="```lua",[11]="if not _warp_mouse then",[12]="    local jettison = fetch\"/system/lib/jettison.lua\" -- Store a copy of jettison.lua",[13]="    store(\"/system/lib/jettison.lua\", \"\") -- Remove the file contents / disable jettison.lua",[14]="    ",[15]="    -- Wait a safe amount of time for the changes to update",[16]="    for i = 1, 5 do flip() end",[17]="    ",[18]="    create_process(pwd()) -- Create a copy of the program (pwd() returns the program's location)",[19]="    ",[20]="    for i = 1, 5 do flip() end -- Ensure the process has been created with the non-existent jettison.",[21]="    ",[22]="    store(\"/system/lib/jettison.lua\", jettison) -- Fix jettison.lua",[23]="    exit() -- Close the program",[24]="end",[25]="```",[26]="",[27]="It may be of use to note that zep writes \"kernel\" as \"kernal\", seen through comments and functions like [`_userland_to_kernal_path`](/picotron_api/functions/kernel/_userland_to_kernal_path/main.md).",[28]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Functions",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="Processes",content={[1]="",[2]="[_ppeek](_ppeek/main.md)",[3]="",[4]="[_ppeek4](_ppeek4/main.md)",[5]="",[6]="[_blit_process_video](_blit_process_video/main.md)",[7]="",[8]="[_env](_env/main.md)",[9]="",[10]="[_kill_process](_kill_process/main.md)",[11]=""},children={}}}},[3]={level=2,text="Files",content={[1]="",[2]="[_cd](_cd/main.md)",[3]="",[4]="[_userland_to_kernal_path](_userland_to_kernal_path/main.md)",[5]="",[6]="[_fstat](_fstat/main.md)",[7]="",[8]="[_fullpath](_fullpath/main.md)",[9]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="Uncategorised",content={[1]="",[2]="[_warp_mouse](_warp_mouse/main.md)",[3]="",[4]="[_apply_system_settings](_apply_system_settings/main.md)",[5]="",[6]="[_pod](_pod_raw/main.md)",[7]="",[8]="[_pod_raw](_pod_raw/main.md)",[9]="",[10]="[_crc](_crc/main.md)"},children={}}}}}}}},[214]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/kernel/_warp_mouse/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="_warp_mouse(x,y)",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="`_warp_mouse` allows for you to move the mouse based on the coordinates `x`,`y`, similar to [mouse()](/picotron_api/functions/mouse/main.md)",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`x`: integer",content={[1]="",[2]="The new mouse's x-coordinate.",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=3,text="`y`: integer",content={[1]="",[2]="The new mouse's y-coordinate."},children={}}}}}}}},[215]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/kernel/_userland_to_kernal_path/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="_userland_to_kernal_path(path, [mode])",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="`_userland_to_kernal_path` converts a userland path, e.g: `/foo.txt` to a kernal path and returns the kernal path.",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`path`: string",content={[1]="",[2]="The kernel path of the file.",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=3,text="`[mode]`: string",content={[1]="",[2]="The mode for the path",[3]=""},children={[1]={level=4,text="One of four:",content={[1]="",[2]="* `R` Read-only",[3]="* `RW` Read-Write",[4]="* `W` Write-only",[5]="* `X` Unknown"},children={}}}}}}}}}},[216]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/kernel/_ppeek4/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="_ppeek4(processid,address)",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="`_ppeek4` allows for a process to peek into the memory of another process, returning the 4 consecutive bytes stored at the memory address `address` and the proceeding 3 bytes in the process under the PID `processid`.",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`processid`: integer",content={[1]="",[2]="The process ID of the process to fetch from.",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=3,text="`address`: integer",content={[1]="",[2]="The memory address (max: 65536) of the process to fetch from.",[3]=""},children={}}}},[3]={level=2,text="Returns",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`bytes`: integer",content={[1]="",[2]="The 4 bytes that were fetched."},children={}}}}}}}},[217]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/kernel/_ppeek/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="_ppeek(processid,address)",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="`_ppeek`, also known as process peek, allows for a process to peek into the memory of another process, returning the data stored at the memory address `address` in the process under the PID `processid`.",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`processid`: integer",content={[1]="",[2]="The process ID of the process to fetch from.",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=3,text="`address`: integer",content={[1]="",[2]="The memory address (max: 65536) of the process to fetch from.",[3]=""},children={}}}},[3]={level=2,text="Returns",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`byte`: integer",content={[1]="",[2]="The byte at address `address` in process with the process id `processid`."},children={}}}}}}}},[218]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/kernel/_pod_raw/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="_pod_raw(obj, [flags], [meta_str])",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="`_pod_raw` acts nearly identically to [`pod()`](/picotron_api/functions/pod/main.md), encoding lua values; excluding functions.",[3]="",[4]="`_pod` is an alias for this function.",[5]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`obj`: any",content={[1]="",[2]="Any data type,",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=3,text="`flags`: integer",content={[1]="",[2]="The flags to be used when podding.",[3]=""},children={[1]={level=4,text="Available Flags:",content={[1]="",[2]="* 0x01 pxu encode userdata in a compressed (RLE-style) form",[3]="* 0x02 lz4 binary compression pass (dictionary matching)",[4]="* 0x04 base64 text encoding (convert back into a text-friendly format)",[5]="* 0x08 (reserved for future use -- should be 0)",[6]="* 0x10 pxu option: store raw (smaller for already-compressed userdata)",[7]="* 0x20 strings option: store raw binary, instead of escaped plain-text",[8]="* 0x40 (reserved for future use -- should be 0)",[9]="* 0x80 pxu option: store in a stable rle format (used for undo stacks)",[10]=""},children={}}}},[3]={level=3,text="`[meta_str]`: string",content={[1]="",[2]="Metadata stored as a string. This is generated in `/system/lib/fs.lua` with:",[3]="",[4]="```lua",[5]="local function _generate_meta_str(meta_p)",[6]="",[7]="	-- use a copy so that can remove pod_format without sideffect",[8]="	-- _pod is wrapped version -- protects again circularities",[9]="	local meta = _unpod(_pod(meta_p)) or {}",[10]="",[11]="	local meta_str = \"--[[\"",[12]="",[13]="	if (meta.pod_format and type(meta.pod_format) == \"string\") then",[14]="		meta_str ..= \"pod_format=\\\"\"\"..meta.pod_format..\"\\\"\"\"",[15]="		meta.pod_format = nil -- don't write twice",[16]="	elseif (meta.pod_type and type(meta.pod_type) == \"string\") then",[17]="		meta_str ..= \"pod_type=\\\"\"\"..meta.pod_type..\"\\\"\"\"",[18]="		meta.pod_type = nil -- don't write twice",[19]="	else",[20]="		meta_str ..= \"pod\"",[21]="	end",[22]="",[23]="	local meta_str1 = _pod(meta, 0x0) -- 0x0: metadata always plain text. want to read it!",[24]="",[25]="	if (meta_str1 and #meta_str1 > 2) then",[26]="		meta_str1 = sub(meta_str1, 2, #meta_str1-1) -- remove {}",[27]="		meta_str ..= \",\"",[28]="		meta_str ..= meta_str1",[29]="	end",[30]="",[31]="	meta_str..=\"]]\"",[32]="",[33]="	return meta_str",[34]="end",[35]="```",[36]=""},children={}}}},[3]={level=2,text="Returns",content={[1]="",[2]="The podded form of `obj`."},children={}}}}}},[219]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/kernel/_kill_process/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="_kill_process(processid)",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="`_kill_process` closes the process with the id `processid`.",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`processid`: integer",content={[1]="",[2]="The process id of the process you want to kill."},children={}}}}}}}},[220]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/kernel/_fullpath/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="_fullpath(kernel_path)",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="`_fullpath` returns the full kernel path of the kernel path `kernel_path`.",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`kernel_path`: string",content={[1]="",[2]="The kernel path of the path to get the full path of.",[3]=""},children={}}}},[3]={level=2,text="Returns",content={[1]="",[2]="The kernel path of the path `kernel_path`."},children={}}}}}},[221]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/kernel/_fstat/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="_fstat(kernel_path)",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="`_fstat` returns the file status of the kernel path, `kernel_path`.",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`kernel_path`: string",content={[1]="",[2]="The kernel path to fetch the file status of.",[3]="",[4]="This can not have a protocol, e.g: `bbs://`.",[5]=""},children={}}}},[3]={level=2,text="Returns",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`filestatus`: string",content={[1]="",[2]="The file status of the kernel path."},children={}}}}}}}},[222]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/kernel/_env/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="_env()",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="`_env` is an alias for [env()](/picotron_api/functions/env/main.md) and returns a table of environment variables given to the process at the time of creation.",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Returns",content={[1]="",[2]="Returns a table of environment variables given to the process at the time of creation."},children={}}}}}},[223]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/kernel/_crc/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="_crc(string)",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="`_crc` returns the hash of string `string`, where each character corresponds to a single byte",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`string`: string",content={[1]="",[2]="The string to generate a hash for.",[3]=""},children={}}}},[3]={level=2,text="Returns",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`crc32hash`: integer",content={[1]="",[2]="The hash of string `string` in bytes, where each character of `string` corresponds to a single byte."},children={}}}}}}}},[224]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/kernel/_cd/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="_cd(kernel_path, [protocol?])",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="`_cd` acts similarly to [cd()](/picotron_api/functions/cd/main.md).",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`kernel_path`: string",content={[1]="",[2]="The kernel path of the file.",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=3,text="`[protocol?]`: boolean",content={[1]="",[2]="Assumed to be whether the path is found at a protocol, rather than locally."},children={}}}}}}}},[225]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/kernel/_blit_process_video/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="_blit_process_video(processid,x,y,[width],[height],x0,y0)",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="`_blit_process_video` allows you to blit the video/screen of a different process to a draw target.",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`processid`: integer",content={[1]="",[2]="The process ID of the process to fetch the video from.",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=3,text="`x`: integer",content={[1]="",[2]="The location in the draw target to blit the video from the other process.",[3]=""},children={}},[3]={level=3,text="`y`: integer",content={[1]="",[2]="The location in the draw target to blit the video from the other process.",[3]=""},children={}},[4]={level=3,text="`[width]`: integer",content={[1]="",[2]="Width of the video to capture.",[3]="",[4]="This seems incorrect, and instead requires being the amount of pixels in the canvas, e.g: `480*270` through `width*height` or it wraps weirdly.",[5]=""},children={}},[5]={level=3,text="`[height]`: integer",content={[1]="",[2]="Height of the video to capture",[3]="",[4]="This seems incorrect, instead requiring to be `1` and then later mutated to be correct.",[5]=""},children={}},[6]={level=3,text="`x0`: integer",content={[1]="",[2]="Top left x-coordinate to capture from the process.",[3]=""},children={}},[7]={level=3,text="`y0`: integer",content={[1]="",[2]="Top left y-coordinate to capture from the process.",[3]=""},children={}}}},[3]={level=2,text="Examples",content={[1]="",[2]="This stores the video of process 3 (wm), which would be the picotron screen, to `/foo.png`.",[3]="",[4]="Snippet from @Soupster (found in the Picotron discord)",[5]="",[6]="```lua",[7]="local ud = userdata(\"u8\", 480*270, 1) --create a userdata u8 image",[8]="",[9]="set_draw_target(ud) -- set draw functions to target this userdata",[10]="_blit_process_video(3, 0, 0, 480*270, 1, 0, 0) --blit the video from process 3 to the userdata",[11]="set_draw_target() -- reset draw target to default",[12]="",[13]="ud:mutate(\"u8\", 480, 270) -- mutate the u8 to be in a bitmap",[14]="store(\"/foo.png\", ud) -- store the u8 as an image (png)",[15]="```"},children={}}}}}},[226]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/kernel/_apply_system_settings/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="_apply_system_settings()",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="Applies the system settings from `/appdata/system/settings.pod`"},children={}}}}}},[227]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/input/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="input([prompt],[flags])",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="`input` is used to allow terminal programs to be interactive; this is a blocking function and stalls the program until the user enters a response.",[3]="",[4]="The response of the user is returned.",[5]="",[6]="Interactive terminal programs that run indefinitely do not have a [_update](/picotron_api/program_structure/main.md) or [_draw](/picotron_api/program_structure/main.md) callback, and instead can use input() in the mainloop.",[7]="",[8]="",[9]="Note that there is currently no way to respond to non-textinput keys (like cursors) from terminal programs.",[10]="",[11]="Terminal programs can be run during development with ctrl-r, but as they occupy the same process as the terminal that is displaying them; there may be some disparities in behaviour.",[12]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`[prompt]`: string",content={[1]="",[2]="The string that shows to the left of the user's input, default: \"?\"",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=3,text="`[flags]`: bitfield",content={[1]="",[2]="A bitfield that can change how inputs work.",[3]="",[4]="Available flags:",[5]="",[6]="* 0x1 hide the prompt once input() has returned",[7]="* 0x2 complete the input when a single character is given (do not wait for enter)",[8]="* 0x4 do not block -- return nil when there is no response that frame",[9]=""},children={}}}},[3]={level=2,text="Returns",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`response`: string",content={[1]="",[2]="The user's response to the input",[3]=""},children={}}}},[4]={level=2,text="Example",content={[1]="",[2]="Sourced from the [Picotron Manual](https://www.lexaloffle.com/dl/docs/picotron_manual.html)",[3]="",[4]="```lua",[5]="print(\"press k,l to adjust value or q to quit\")",[6]="print(\"\") -- is going to get consumed by \\\"r below",[7]="val = 10",[8]="while true do",[9]="    res = input(\"\",0x7)",[10]="    if (res == \"q\") exit()",[11]="    if (res == \"k\") val -= 1",[12]="    if (res == \"l\") val += 1",[13]="    print(\"\\\"rvalue: \"..val) -- \\\"r to write over previous line",[14]="end",[15]="print(\"finished\")",[16]="```"},children={}}}}}},[228]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/include/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="include(filename)",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="The `include` function allows you to attach scripting files to your program (`lua`).",[3]="",[4]="If the source has a syntax error, it reports errors to the console, listing the syntax error and location",[5]="If the source file is not a text file, it notifies that it cannot include it.",[6]="",[7]="Source: [source.lua](source.lua)",[8]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="filename",content={[1]="",[2]="The relative or absolute path to the file.",[3]=""},children={}}}},[3]={level=2,text="Examples",content={[1]="",[2]="Example `module.lua` which returns a version number and creates two helper functions for drawing sprites.",[3]="",[4]="```lua",[5]="function sprFlippedX(s,x,y)",[6]="    spr(s,x,y,true)",[7]="end",[8]="",[9]="function sprFlippedY(s,x,y)",[10]="    spr(s,x,y,nil,true)",[11]="end",[12]="",[13]="return \"1.0\"",[14]="```",[15]="",[16]="Cartridge that includes this",[17]="",[18]="```lua",[19]="local version=include(\"module.lua\")",[20]="if (version != latestVersion){",[21]="    notify(\"module.lua is not the running the latest version.\")",[22]="}",[23]="",[24]="sprFlippedX(1,0,0)",[25]="```",[26]="",[27]="This warns you if the version is not the latest version (e.g: taken from a webpage), then proceeds to draw sprite 1 at 0,0 using the helper function.",[28]=""},children={}},[4]={level=2,text="Returns",content={[1]="",[2]="Returns `func()`, so that the imported file can return something - this also runs the file with `func()`",[3]="256 file limit > `nil`",[4]="Can't fetch the file > `nil`",[5]=""},children={}},[5]={level=2,text="Limitations",content={[1]="",[2]="There is an arbritrary limit where only 256 of the same files can be included."},children={}}}}}},[229]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/get_spr/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="get_spr(index)",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="`get_spr` returns the sprite information of the sprite at `index`.",[3]="",[4]="Source: [source.lua](source.lua)",[5]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="index",content={[1]="",[2]="The sprite index of which you want the information",[3]=""},children={}}}},[3]={level=2,text="Returns",content={[1]="",[2]="This returns the `userdata u8` graphical data of the sprite at sprite `index`."},children={}}}}}},[230]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/get_draw_target/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="get_draw_target()",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="Returns the current draw target; a 2d userdata u8.",[3]="",[4]="[set_draw_target()](/picotron_api/functions/set_draw_target/main.md) sets the draw target.",[5]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Returns",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`draw_target`: userdata",content={[1]="",[2]="The current draw target; a 2d userdata u8."},children={}}}}}}}},[231]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/get_display/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="get_display()",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="Returns the current display as a u8, 2d userdata.",[3]="",[4]="You cannot set the display userdata directly; it may be resized with the [window()](/picotron_api/functions/window/main.md) function.",[5]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Returns",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`display`: userdata",content={[1]="",[2]="The current display as a u8, 2d userdata."},children={}}}}}}}},[232]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/get_clipboard/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="get_clipboard()",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="`get_clipboard` fetches the user's clipboard.",[3]="",[4]="For security reasons, get_clipboard() only has access to the host clipboard after ctrl-v is pressed while Picotron is active.",[5]="",[6]="Until ctrl-v is pressed, changes to the host clipboard have no effect on the return value of get_clipboard().",[7]="",[8]="The same is true for sandboxed applications (e.g. bbs carts); they are only able to access clipboard contents from other processes once ctrl-v is pressed while that app has keyboard focus.",[9]="",[10]="This is typically used in conjunction with [set_clipboard()](/picotron_api/functions/set_clipboard/main.md).",[11]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Returns",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`clipboard`: string",content={[1]="",[2]="The clipboard that is fetched from the user.",[3]=""},children={}}}},[3]={level=2,text="Examples",content={[1]="",[2]="Sourced from the [Picotron Manual](https://www.lexaloffle.com/dl/docs/picotron_manual.html)",[3]="",[4]="```lua",[5]="out = \"[output]\\\"n\"",[6]="",[7]="function _update()",[8]="",[9]="    if key\"ctrl\" and keyp\"c\" then",[10]="        local test_str = \"test\"..flr(rnd(10000))",[11]="        set_clipboard(test_str)",[12]="        out ..= \"ctrl-c copied: \"..test_str..\"\\\"n\"",[13]="    end",[14]="",[15]="    if key\"ctrl\" and keyp\"v\" then",[16]="        out ..= \"ctrl-v pasted: \"..get_clipboard()..\"\\\"n\"",[17]="    end",[18]="",[19]="    -- this will only work for clipboard contents that is copied from within Picotron ",[20]="    -- (or within the same app when sandboxed), unless pasted with ctrl-v first.",[21]="    if key\"ctrl\" and keyp\"b\" then",[22]="        out ..= \"ctrl-b pasted: \"..get_clipboard()..\"\\\"n\"",[23]="    end",[24]="end",[25]="",[26]="function _draw()",[27]="    cls()",[28]="    print(out, 2,2,7)",[29]="end",[30]="```"},children={}}}}}},[233]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/fullpath/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="fullpath(filename)",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="`fullpath` resolves a filename to its canonical path based on the present working directory.",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Returns",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`path`: string",content={[1]="",[2]="The canonical path of the file."},children={}}}}}}}},[234]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/fstat/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="fstat(filename)",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="`fstat` returns information about the requested file: the type, size and the origin of the file.",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`filename`: string",content={[1]="",[2]="The file you want to get information about",[3]=""},children={}}}},[3]={level=2,text="Returns",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`type`: string",content={[1]="",[2]="Whether the path is a `file` or `folder`",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=3,text="`size`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="The size of the file (bytes)",[3]=""},children={}},[3]={level=3,text="`origin`: string",content={[1]="",[2]="The origin of the file",[3]=""},children={}}}}}}}},[235]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/fset/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="fset(n,f,active)",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="Set flag `f` of sprite `n` to boolean `active`.",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`n`: integer",content={[1]="The sprite index.",[2]=""},children={}},[2]={level=3,text="`f`: integer",content={[1]="The flag index 0..7",[2]=""},children={}},[3]={level=3,text="`active`: boolean",content={[1]="Boolean to set the flag to.",[2]=""},children={}}}}}},[2]={level=1,text="fset(n,b)",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="Set flags of sprite `n` to bitfield `b`.",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`n`: integer",content={[1]="The sprite index.",[2]=""},children={}},[2]={level=3,text="`b`: bitfield",content={[1]="Boolean to set the flags of sprite index to.",[2]=""},children={}}}},[3]={level=2,text="Examples",content={[1]="",[2]="```lua",[3]="fset(2, 1 | 2 | 8)   -- sets bits 0,1 and 3",[4]="fset(2, 4, true) -- sets bit 4",[5]="print(fget(2))   -- 27 (1 | 2 | 8 | 16)",[6]="```"},children={}}}}}},[236]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/foreach/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="foreach(table,callback)",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="Iterate over the table `table` and call the callback function `callback` for each element.",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`table`: table",content={[1]="",[2]="The table you would like to iterate through.",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=3,text="`callback`: function",content={[1]="",[2]="The function to callback for each element; this gets called in the form of",[3]="",[4]="```lua",[5]="callback(element_value)",[6]="```",[7]=""},children={}}}},[3]={level=2,text="Examples",content={[1]="",[2]="Sourced from the [Picotron Manual](https://www.lexaloffle.com/dl/docs/picotron_manual.html)",[3]="",[4]="```lua",[5]="foreach({1,2,3}, print)",[6]="```"},children={}}}}}},[237]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/fillp/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="fillp(p)",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="Set a 4x4 fill pattern using PICO-8 style fill patterns.",[3]="",[4]="`p` is a bitfield in reading order starting from the highest bit.",[5]="",[6]="This affects [circ](/picotron_api/functions/circ/main.md), [circfill](/picotron_api/functions/circfill/main.md), [oval](/picotron_api/functions/oval/main.md), [ovalfill](/picotron_api/functions/ovalfill/main.md), [rect](/picotron_api/functions/rect/main.md), [rectfill](/picotron_api/functions/rectfill/main.md), [pset](/picotron_api/functions/pset/main.md), [line](/picotron_api/functions/line/main.md).",[7]="",[8]="Fill patterns in Picotron are 64-bit specified 8 bytes from 0x5500, where each byte is a row (top to bottom) and the low bit is on the left.",[9]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`p`: bitfield",content={[1]="",[2]="A bitfield in reading order starting from the highest bit",[3]=""},children={}}}},[3]={level=2,text="Examples",content={[1]="",[2]="Sourced from the [Picotron Manual](https://www.lexaloffle.com/dl/docs/picotron_manual.html)",[3]="",[4]="Draw a red (colour 8) rounded rectangle 40 pixels wide and 30 pixels talls with 3 pixels missing at each corner (radius 2):",[5]="```lua",[6]="rrectfill(100,50,40,30,2,8)",[7]="```",[8]=""},children={}}}},[2]={level=1,text="fillp(1,2,3,4,5,6,7,8)",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="Sets a 8x8 fill pattern.",[3]="This affects [circ](/picotron_api/functions/circ/main.md), [circfill](/picotron_api/functions/circfill/main.md), [oval](/picotron_api/functions/oval/main.md), [ovalfill](/picotron_api/functions/ovalfill/main.md), [rect](/picotron_api/functions/rect/main.md), [rectfill](/picotron_api/functions/rectfill/main.md), [pset](/picotron_api/functions/pset/main.md), [line](/picotron_api/functions/line/main.md).",[4]="",[5]="To define an 8x8 with high bits on the right (so that binary numbers visually match), fillp can be called with 8 arguments.",[6]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=4,text="`1`: bitfield",content={[1]="",[2]="Bitfield with high bits on the right",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=4,text="`2`: bitfield",content={[1]="",[2]="Bitfield with high bits on the right",[3]=""},children={}},[3]={level=4,text="`3`: bitfield",content={[1]="",[2]="Bitfield with high bits on the right",[3]=""},children={}},[4]={level=4,text="`4`: bitfield",content={[1]="",[2]="Bitfield with high bits on the right",[3]=""},children={}},[5]={level=4,text="`5`: bitfield",content={[1]="",[2]="Bitfield with high bits on the right",[3]=""},children={}},[6]={level=4,text="`6`: bitfield",content={[1]="",[2]="Bitfield with high bits on the right",[3]=""},children={}},[7]={level=4,text="`7`: bitfield",content={[1]="",[2]="Bitfield with high bits on the right",[3]=""},children={}},[8]={level=4,text="`8`: bitfield",content={[1]="",[2]="Bitfield with high bits on the right",[3]=""},children={}}}}}},[2]={level=2,text="Examples",content={[1]="",[2]="Sourced from the [Picotron Manual](https://www.lexaloffle.com/dl/docs/picotron_manual.html)",[3]="",[4]="```lua",[5]="fillp(",[6]="    0b10000000,",[7]="    0b01011110,",[8]="    0b00101110,",[9]="    0b00010110,",[10]="    0b00001010,",[11]="    0b00000100,",[12]="    0b00000010,",[13]="    0b00000001",[14]=")",[15]="```"},children={}}}}}},[238]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/fget/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="fget(n,f)",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="Retrieve the flag(s) of sprite `n`.",[3]="",[4]="The initial state of flags 0..7 are settable in the sprite editor, so can be used to create custom sprite attributes."},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`n`: integer",content={[1]="The sprite index.",[2]=""},children={}},[2]={level=3,text="`f`: integer",content={[1]="The flag index 0..7",[2]=""},children={}}}},[3]={level=2,text="Returns",content={[1]="Flag `f` as a boolean of sprite `n`",[2]=""},children={}}}},[2]={level=1,text="fset(n)",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="Retrieve the flag bitfield of sprite `n`.",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`n`: integer",content={[1]="The sprite index.",[2]=""},children={}}}},[3]={level=2,text="Returns",content={[1]="The bitfield of flags of sprite `n`.",[2]=""},children={}},[4]={level=2,text="Examples",content={[1]="",[2]="```lua",[3]="fset(2, 1 | 2 | 8)   -- sets bits 0,1 and 3",[4]="fset(2, 4, true) -- sets bit 4",[5]="print(fget(2))   -- 27 (1 | 2 | 8 | 16)",[6]="```"},children={}}}}}},[239]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/fetch_metadata/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="fetch_metadata(filename)",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="`fetch_metadata` fetches the (picotron) metadata of a file; this can be from a local path (if it is accessible from the picotron drive) and online.",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`filename`: string",content={[1]="",[2]="The filename to fetch from.",[3]="",[4]="This can be from online, e.g: from the `bbs://`, `http://` or `https://` protocols.",[5]=""},children={}}}},[3]={level=2,text="Returns",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`meta`: table",content={[1]="",[2]="The metadata of the requested file"},children={}}}}}}}},[240]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/fetch/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="fetch(filename,[options])",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="`fetch` fetches a file; this can be from a local path (if it is accessible from the picotron drive) and online.",[3]="",[4]="Unless this is an online fetch with `on_complete` in the options, it is a blocking function and stalls the process.",[5]="",[6]="This returns the filedata and the metadata.",[7]="",[8]="When running under web, `/appdata` (and only `/appdata`) is persisted using Indexed DB storage.",[9]="",[10]="This applies to both html exports and carts running on the BBS.",[11]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`filename`: string",content={[1]="",[2]="The filename to fetch from.",[3]="",[4]="This can be from online, e.g: from the `bbs://`, `http://` or `https://` protocols.",[5]=""},children={}},[2]={level=3,text="`[options]`: table",content={[1]="",[2]="The available options are:",[3]=""},children={[1]={level=4,text="`on_complete`: function",content={[1]="",[2]="This is for online fetches only.",[3]="",[4]="A callback method that is called with the filedata when it fetches; this makes fetch non-blocking and asynchronous.",[5]=""},children={}},[2]={level=4,text="`raw_str`: boolean",content={[1]="",[2]="Whether to return the raw string of a file; this can fix some files that can fail to fetch when they have picotron metadata, e.g: `.p8` files.",[3]=""},children={}},[3]={level=4,text="`argb`: boolean",content={[1]="",[2]="Whether to return the raw picture data of a `.png`; the same that it used to before `png` fetches converted into [`userdata u8`](/picotron_api/userdata/) automatically.",[3]=""},children={}}}}}},[3]={level=2,text="Returns",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`filedata`: any|nil",content={[1]="",[2]="The filedata of the file.",[3]="",[4]="This does not return anything when `[options].on_complete` is present as it becomes asynchronous.",[5]=""},children={}},[2]={level=3,text="`meta`: table",content={[1]="",[2]="The metadata of the file; seems to fail with local fetches.",[3]="",[4]="This does not return anything when `[options].on_complete` is present as it becomes asynchronous."},children={}}}}}}}},[241]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/exit/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="exit(unknown)",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="`exit` immediately closes the program; this can be used to terminate a terminal program early.",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`unknown`: unknown",content={[1]="",[2]="Argument observed; seemingly useless.",[3]=""},children={}}}},[3]={level=2,text="Examples",content={[1]="",[2]="Sourced from the [Picotron Manual](https://www.lexaloffle.com/dl/docs/picotron_manual.html)",[3]="",[4]="```lua",[5]="s = input(\"would you like to continue? [y/n] \")",[6]="if (s == \"n\") exit(0)",[7]="```"},children={}}}}}},[242]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/error/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="error([description],[unknown])",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="`error` causes a crash with a description (a string) at the top of the stack trace.",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`[description]`: string",content={[1]="",[2]="The description to show at the top of the stack trace.",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=3,text="`[unknown]`: unknown",content={[1]="",[2]="Seen as an argument; what it does is unknown; seemingly useless."},children={}}}}}}}},[243]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/env/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="env()",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="This returns a list of environment variables given to the process, given through [`create_process`](/picotron_api/functions/create_process/main.md).",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Returns",content={[1]="",[2]="Returns the table of environment variables."},children={}}}}}},[244]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/deli/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="deli(table,[index]): any",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="`deli` deletes the element at index `[index]` in the table `table`, defaulting to the last element of the table.",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`table`: table",content={[1]="",[2]="The table you would like to delete from.",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=3,text="`[index]`: integer",content={[1]="",[2]="The index of the element to delete from the table `table`.",[3]=""},children={}}}},[3]={level=2,text="Returns",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="element: any",content={[1]="",[2]="Returns the element that was deleted"},children={}}}}}}}},[245]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/del/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="del(table,value)",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="`del` deletes the value `value` from the table `table`.",[3]="Without an index, it is equivalent to:",[4]="",[5]="```lua",[6]="tbl[#tbl + 1] = val",[7]="```",[8]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`table`: table",content={[1]="",[2]="The table you would like to add `value` to.",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=3,text="`value`: any",content={[1]="",[2]="The value to add into the table `table`",[3]=""},children={}},[3]={level=3,text="[index]: integer",content={[1]="",[2]="The index of where to place the item in the table `table`, defaults to the end of the table.",[3]=""},children={}}}},[3]={level=2,text="Examples",content={[1]="",[2]="Sourced from the [Picotron Manual](https://www.lexaloffle.com/dl/docs/picotron_manual.html)",[3]="",[4]="```lua",[5]="foo={}-- create empty table",[6]="add(foo, 11)",[7]="add(foo, 22)",[8]="?foo[2] -- 22",[9]="```"},children={}}}}}},[246]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/date/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="date([format],[t],[delta]): string",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="Returns the current day and time formatted using Lua's standard date strings.",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`[format]`: string",content={[1]="",[2]="The output string format; defaults to \"!%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S\" (UTC).",[3]="",[4]="Picotron timestamps use this default format.",[5]=""},children={}},[2]={level=3,text="`[t]`: string|number",content={[1]="",[2]="The moment in time to be encoded in a string; defaults to the current time.",[3]="",[4]="When a string: the date in format \"!%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S\" (UTC)",[5]="",[6]="When an integer: the epoch timestamp",[7]=""},children={}},[3]={level=3,text="`[delta]`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="The amount of seconds to add to `[t]`",[3]=""},children={}}}},[3]={level=2,text="Returns",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`date`: string",content={[1]="",[2]="The date requested in the requested format",[3]=""},children={}}}},[4]={level=2,text="Examples",content={[1]="",[2]="Sourced from the [Picotron Manual](https://www.lexaloffle.com/dl/docs/picotron_manual.html)",[3]="",[4]="Show the current UTC time (use this for timestamps)",[5]="```lua",[6]="?date()",[7]="```",[8]="",[9]="Show the current local time",[10]="```lua",[11]="?date(\"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S\")",[12]="```",[13]="convert a UTC date to local time",[14]="```lua",[15]="?date(\"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S\", \"2024-03-14 03:14:00\")",[16]="```",[17]="",[18]="local time 1 hour ago",[19]="```lua",[20]="?date(\"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S\", nil, -60*60)",[21]="```",[22]="",[23]=""},children={}}}}}},[247]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/cursor/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="cursor(x,y,[col])",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="`cursor` sets the cursor position to `x`,`y`.",[3]="",[4]="If `[col]` is set, it also sets the color of the cursor.",[5]="",[6]="This is modifying the text cursor, *not* the mouse pointer; for that, see [mouse()](/picotron_api/functions/mouse/main.md)",[7]=""},children={}},[2]={level=2,text="Arguments",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=3,text="`x`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="The new x-coordinate of the cursor.",[3]=""},children={}},[2]={level=3,text="`y`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="The new y-coordinate of the cursor.",[3]=""},children={}},[3]={level=3,text="`[col]`: number",content={[1]="",[2]="The new color of the cursor."},children={}}}}}}}},[248]={name="main.md",path="/picotron_api/functions/create_process/main.md",data={[1]={level=1,text="create_process(prog_name_p, [env_patch])",content={[1]=""},children={[1]={level=2,text="Overview",content={[1]="",[2]="`create_process` runs a process, `prog_name_p`, with optional env data patched in through
